-- Cadastro de veículos/anos para amortecedores e molas; tipo apenas para amortecedores.
-- Aplicar uma vez, depois de 202610240003_springs_without_testing.sql.
begin;

alter table public.products add column shock_type text
  check (shock_type is null or (
    shock_type in ('gas_pressurized', 'conventional_hydraulic', 'adjustable')
    and split_part(code, '-', 1) not in ('MGM', 'MFI', 'MRE', 'MFR')
  ));

create table public.product_applications (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  product_id uuid not null references public.products(id) on delete cascade,
  vehicle_model text not null check (length(trim(vehicle_model)) between 1 and 120),
  year_from integer check (year_from between 1 and 9999),
  year_to integer check (year_to between 1 and 9999),
  position integer not null check (position >= 0),
  check (year_from is null or year_to is null or year_from <= year_to),
  unique (product_id, position)
);

create unique index product_applications_no_duplicates
  on public.product_applications (product_id, lower(trim(vehicle_model)), coalesce(year_from, 0), coalesce(year_to, 0));

alter table public.product_applications enable row level security;
revoke all on public.product_applications from anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.product_applications to authenticated;
create policy product_applications_read_active_users
  on public.product_applications for select to authenticated
  using ((select private.current_user_role()) is not null);

-- Atualiza o conjunto de aplicações de uma vez. Somente administradores podem salvar.
create or replace function public.update_product_details(
  p_product_code text,
  p_shock_type text,
  p_applications jsonb,
  p_expected_updated_at timestamptz
)
returns timestamptz
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_product public.products%rowtype;
  v_updated_at timestamptz;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is distinct from 'administrator'::public.app_role then
    raise exception 'Somente administradores alteram o cadastro de aplicações.' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  select * into v_product from public.products
  where code = upper(trim(p_product_code)) for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'Produto não encontrado.' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  if p_expected_updated_at is null or v_product.updated_at is distinct from p_expected_updated_at then
    raise exception 'O cadastro mudou desde a abertura. Atualize a página antes de salvar.' using errcode = '40001';
  end if;
  if p_shock_type is not null and (
    p_shock_type not in ('gas_pressurized', 'conventional_hydraulic', 'adjustable')
    or split_part(v_product.code, '-', 1) in ('MGM', 'MFI', 'MRE', 'MFR')
  ) then
    raise exception 'Tipo de amortecedor inválido para este produto.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if jsonb_typeof(p_applications) is distinct from 'array' then
    raise exception 'As aplicações devem ser uma lista.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if jsonb_array_length(p_applications) > 50 then
    raise exception 'Informe até 50 aplicações por produto.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if exists (
    select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(p_applications) as item
    where jsonb_typeof(item) is distinct from 'object'
       or jsonb_typeof(item -> 'vehicle_model') is distinct from 'string'
  ) then
    raise exception 'Cada aplicação precisa conter o nome do veículo.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if exists (
    select 1 from jsonb_to_recordset(p_applications) as item(vehicle_model text, year_from integer, year_to integer)
    where length(trim(coalesce(item.vehicle_model, ''))) not between 1 and 120
       or (item.year_from is not null and item.year_from not between 1 and 9999)
       or (item.year_to is not null and item.year_to not between 1 and 9999)
       or item.year_from > item.year_to
  ) then
    raise exception 'Confira o veículo e o intervalo de anos.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if exists (
    select 1 from jsonb_to_recordset(p_applications) as item(vehicle_model text, year_from integer, year_to integer)
    group by lower(trim(item.vehicle_model)), item.year_from, item.year_to having count(*) > 1
  ) then
    raise exception 'Há aplicações repetidas na lista.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  delete from public.product_applications where product_id = v_product.id;
  insert into public.product_applications (product_id, vehicle_model, year_from, year_to, position)
  select v_product.id, trim(item.value ->> 'vehicle_model'),
         (item.value ->> 'year_from')::integer, (item.value ->> 'year_to')::integer,
         (item.ordinality - 1)::integer
  from jsonb_array_elements(p_applications) with ordinality as item(value, ordinality);

  v_updated_at := clock_timestamp();
  update public.products set shock_type = p_shock_type, updated_at = v_updated_at where id = v_product.id;
  return v_updated_at;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.update_product_details(text, text, jsonb, timestamptz) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.update_product_details(text, text, jsonb, timestamptz) to authenticated;

create or replace function public.inventory_api_version()
returns integer language sql immutable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$ select 3 $$;

commit;

-- Histórico de alterações feitas pelo cadastro de aplicações do site.
-- Aplicar uma vez, depois de 202610240004_product_applications.sql.
begin;

lock table public.products, public.product_applications in share row exclusive mode;

  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  product_id uuid references public.products(id) on delete set null,
  product_code text not null,

-- VOLATILE permite enxergar as alterações feitas antes da chamada nesta transação.
create function private.product_application_snapshot(p_product_id uuid)
returns jsonb language sql volatile security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'shockType', product.shock_type,
    'applications', coalesce((
      select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
        'vehicleModel', application.vehicle_model,
        'yearFrom', application.year_from,
        'yearTo', application.year_to
      ) order by application.position)
      from public.product_applications as application
      where application.product_id = product.id
    ), '[]'::jsonb)
  )
  from public.products as product where product.id = p_product_id
$$;
revoke all on function private.product_application_snapshot(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- A situação existente é um ponto de partida; não inventamos datas ou autores de edições passadas.
insert into public.product_application_history (product_id, product_code, change_kind, details_after)
select product.id, product.code, 'baseline', private.product_application_snapshot(product.id)
from public.products as product
where split_part(product.code, '-', 1) not in ('MGM', 'MFI', 'MRE', 'MFR')
  and (product.shock_type is not null or exists (
    select 1 from public.product_applications as application where application.product_id = product.id
  ));

-- Preserva toda a validação existente dentro de uma função interna, fora da API pública.
alter function public.update_product_details(text, text, jsonb, timestamptz) set schema private;
alter function private.update_product_details(text, text, jsonb, timestamptz) rename to update_product_details_core;
revoke all on function private.update_product_details_core(text, text, jsonb, timestamptz) from public, anon, authenticated;

-- Cadastro e auditoria são gravados juntos: se o histórico falhar, a edição é cancelada.
create function public.update_product_details(
  p_product_code text,
  p_shock_type text,
  p_applications jsonb,
  p_expected_updated_at timestamptz
)
returns timestamptz
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_product_id uuid;
  v_product_code text;
  v_before jsonb;
  v_after jsonb;
  v_updated_at timestamptz;
  v_actor_name text;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is distinct from 'administrator'::public.app_role then
    raise exception 'Somente administradores alteram o cadastro de aplicações.' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  select id, code into v_product_id, v_product_code from public.products
  where code = upper(trim(p_product_code)) for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'Produto não encontrado.' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;

  v_before := private.product_application_snapshot(v_product_id);
  v_updated_at := private.update_product_details_core(
    p_product_code, p_shock_type, p_applications, p_expected_updated_at
  );
  v_after := private.product_application_snapshot(v_product_id);

  -- Clicar em Salvar sem mudar os valores não gera uma alteração fictícia.
  if v_before is distinct from v_after then
    select display_name into v_actor_name from public.profiles where id = auth.uid();
    insert into public.product_application_history (
      product_id, product_code, change_kind, details_before, details_after,
      performed_by, actor_display_name
    ) values (
      v_product_id, v_product_code, 'updated', v_before, v_after,
      auth.uid(), nullif(trim(v_actor_name), '')
    );
  end if;
  return v_updated_at;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.update_product_details(text, text, jsonb, timestamptz) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.update_product_details(text, text, jsonb, timestamptz) to authenticated;

returns integer language sql immutable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$ select 4 $$;

-- Nome exibido da própria conta. Não permite alterar função, ativação ou outra pessoa.
-- Aplicar uma vez, depois de 202610240005_application_history.sql.
begin;

create function public.update_own_display_name(p_display_name text)
returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_user_id uuid;
  v_name text;
begin
  v_user_id := auth.uid();
  if v_user_id is null or private.current_user_role() is null then
    raise exception 'É necessário entrar com uma conta ativa.' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  v_name := trim(coalesce(p_display_name, ''));
  if length(v_name) not between 1 and 80 then
    raise exception 'Informe um nome entre 1 e 80 caracteres.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  update public.profiles set display_name = v_name
  where id = v_user_id and is_active = true
  returning display_name into v_name;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'O perfil não está ativo.' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return v_name;
end;
$$;

-- Fabricantes/marcas das peças para garantia de amortecedores e molas.
-- Aplicar uma vez, depois de 202610240006_own_display_name.sql.
begin;

create table public.warranty_manufacturers (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  name text not null check (length(trim(name)) between 1 and 100),
  created_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  updated_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create unique index warranty_manufacturers_unique_name on public.warranty_manufacturers (lower(name));

alter table public.warranty_manufacturers enable row level security;
revoke all on public.warranty_manufacturers from public, anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.warranty_manufacturers to authenticated;
create policy warranty_manufacturers_read_active_users
  on public.warranty_manufacturers for select to authenticated
  using ((select private.current_user_role()) is not null);

-- Referências fornecidas pelo usuário; não são montadoras de veículos nem itens em garantia.
insert into public.warranty_manufacturers (name) values ('Moripar'), ('Trebor'), ('New Parts');

create function public.save_warranty_manufacturer(
  p_name text,
  p_id uuid default null,
  p_expected_updated_at timestamptz default null
)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_name text;
  v_record public.warranty_manufacturers%rowtype;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is distinct from 'administrator'::public.app_role then
    raise exception 'Somente administradores gerenciam fabricantes.' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  v_name := regexp_replace(trim(coalesce(p_name, '')), '[[:space:]]+', ' ', 'g');
  if length(v_name) not between 1 and 100 then
    raise exception 'Informe um fabricante entre 1 e 100 caracteres.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  if p_id is null then
    insert into public.warranty_manufacturers (name, created_by, updated_by)
    values (v_name, auth.uid(), auth.uid()) returning * into v_record;
  else
    select * into v_record from public.warranty_manufacturers where id = p_id for update;
    if not found then
      raise exception 'Fabricante não encontrado.' using errcode = 'P0002';
    end if;
    if p_expected_updated_at is null or v_record.updated_at is distinct from p_expected_updated_at then
      raise exception 'O cadastro mudou. Atualize a lista antes de editar.' using errcode = '40001';
    end if;
    update public.warranty_manufacturers
    set name = v_name, updated_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = clock_timestamp()
    where id = p_id returning * into v_record;
  end if;
  return to_jsonb(v_record);
end;
$$;

revoke all on function public.save_warranty_manufacturer(text, uuid, timestamptz) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.save_warranty_manufacturer(text, uuid, timestamptz) to authenticated;

-- Caixas em garantia por fabricante e categoria, separadas do estoque normal.
-- Aplicar uma vez depois da migração 007.
begin;

create table public.warranty_box_balances (
  manufacturer_id uuid not null references public.warranty_manufacturers(id),
  product_kind text not null check (product_kind in ('shock', 'spring')),
  quantity integer not null default 0 check (quantity >= 0),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (manufacturer_id, product_kind)
);
create table public.warranty_box_movements (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  request_id uuid not null unique,
  manufacturer_id uuid not null references public.warranty_manufacturers(id),
  product_kind text not null check (product_kind in ('shock', 'spring')),
  direction text not null check (direction in ('in', 'out')),
  quantity integer not null check (quantity > 0),
  quantity_after integer not null check (quantity_after >= 0),
  note text check (length(note) <= 500),
  performed_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp()
);
create index warranty_box_movements_manufacturer_date
  on public.warranty_box_movements (manufacturer_id, created_at desc);

alter table public.warranty_box_balances enable row level security;
alter table public.warranty_box_movements enable row level security;
revoke all on public.warranty_box_balances, public.warranty_box_movements from public, anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.warranty_box_balances, public.warranty_box_movements to authenticated;
create policy warranty_box_balances_read_active on public.warranty_box_balances
  for select to authenticated using ((select private.current_user_role()) is not null);
create policy warranty_box_movements_read_admin on public.warranty_box_movements
  for select to authenticated using ((select private.current_user_role()) = 'administrator'::public.app_role);

create function public.change_warranty_boxes(
  p_manufacturer_id uuid, p_product_kind text, p_direction text,
  p_quantity integer, p_note text, p_request_id uuid
)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_current integer;
  v_after integer;
  v_note text;
  v_movement public.warranty_box_movements%rowtype;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is distinct from 'administrator'::public.app_role then
    raise exception 'Somente administradores movimentam caixas em garantia nesta etapa.' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_product_kind is null or p_product_kind not in ('shock', 'spring')
     or p_direction is null or p_direction not in ('in', 'out')
     or p_quantity is null or p_quantity < 1 or p_request_id is null then
    raise exception 'Confira a categoria, operação e quantidade inteira de caixas.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  v_note := nullif(trim(coalesce(p_note, '')), '');
  if length(v_note) > 500 then
    raise exception 'A observação deve ter até 500 caracteres.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  -- Serializa operações do mesmo fabricante, inclusive quando o saldo ainda não existe.
  perform 1 from public.warranty_manufacturers where id = p_manufacturer_id for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'Fabricante não encontrado.' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;

  -- Uma repetição da mesma requisição não soma ou desconta caixas duas vezes.
  select * into v_movement from public.warranty_box_movements where request_id = p_request_id;
  if found then
    if v_movement.manufacturer_id is distinct from p_manufacturer_id
       or v_movement.product_kind is distinct from p_product_kind
       or v_movement.direction is distinct from p_direction
       or v_movement.quantity is distinct from p_quantity
       or v_movement.note is distinct from v_note
       or v_movement.performed_by is distinct from auth.uid() then
      raise exception 'Esta identificação já foi usada em outra operação.' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    return to_jsonb(v_movement);
  end if;

  insert into public.warranty_box_balances (manufacturer_id, product_kind)
  values (p_manufacturer_id, p_product_kind) on conflict do nothing;
  select quantity into v_current from public.warranty_box_balances
  where manufacturer_id = p_manufacturer_id and product_kind = p_product_kind for update;
  if p_direction = 'out' and p_quantity > v_current then
    raise exception 'Saldo insuficiente para retirar essa quantidade.' using errcode = 'P0001';
  end if;
  v_after := v_current + case when p_direction = 'in' then p_quantity else -p_quantity end;
  update public.warranty_box_balances set quantity = v_after, updated_at = clock_timestamp()
  where manufacturer_id = p_manufacturer_id and product_kind = p_product_kind;
  insert into public.warranty_box_movements (
    request_id, manufacturer_id, product_kind, direction, quantity, quantity_after, note, performed_by
  ) values (
    p_request_id, p_manufacturer_id, p_product_kind, p_direction, p_quantity, v_after, v_note, auth.uid()
  ) returning * into v_movement;
  return to_jsonb(v_movement);
end;
$$;
revoke all on function public.change_warranty_boxes(uuid, text, text, integer, text, uuid) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.change_warranty_boxes(uuid, text, text, integer, text, uuid) to authenticated;

create or replace function public.inventory_api_version()
returns integer language sql immutable security definer set search_path = ''
as $$ select 7 $$;
commit;

136 linhas · SHA-256 8d89d9537bd07eda

-- Aplicar UMA vez depois de 202610240008_warranty_boxes.sql.
-- Devoluções recebidas não viram estoque para venda automaticamente.
begin;

create table public.warranty_returns (
  id uuid primary key,
  manufacturer_id uuid references public.warranty_manufacturers(id),
  product_kind text not null check (product_kind in ('shock', 'spring', 'unknown')),
  product_code text not null check (length(product_code) <= 60),
  description text not null check (length(trim(description)) between 1 and 300),
  boxes integer not null check (boxes > 0),
  reason text not null check (length(trim(reason)) between 1 and 500),
  location text not null check (length(trim(location)) between 1 and 100),
  ownership text not null check (ownership in ('unknown', 'ours', 'other')),
  status text not null check (status in ('received', 'awaiting_test', 'identification', 'warranty', 'closed')),
  destination text not null check (length(destination) <= 300),
  created_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  updated_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),
  check (status not in ('awaiting_test', 'identification') or (product_kind = 'shock' and ownership = 'ours')),
  check (status <> 'warranty' or (manufacturer_id is not null and product_kind <> 'unknown' and ownership = 'ours')),
  check (status <> 'closed' or length(trim(destination)) > 0)
);
create index warranty_returns_recent on public.warranty_returns (created_at desc);

-- Guarda antes/depois e o responsável. Não cria outro botão de histórico na Garantia.
create table public.warranty_return_events (
  request_id uuid primary key,
  return_id uuid not null references public.warranty_returns(id),
  before_data jsonb,
  after_data jsonb not null,
  performed_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp()
);
alter table public.warranty_returns enable row level security;
alter table public.warranty_return_events enable row level security;
revoke all on public.warranty_returns, public.warranty_return_events from public, anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.warranty_returns, public.warranty_return_events to authenticated;
create policy warranty_returns_read_active on public.warranty_returns for select to authenticated
  using ((select private.current_user_role()) is not null);
create policy warranty_return_events_read_admin on public.warranty_return_events for select to authenticated
  using ((select private.current_user_role()) = 'administrator'::public.app_role);

create function public.save_warranty_return(p_id uuid, p_input jsonb, p_expected_updated_at timestamptz, p_request_id uuid)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_old public.warranty_returns%rowtype;
  v_new public.warranty_returns%rowtype;
  v_event public.warranty_return_events%rowtype;
  v_before jsonb;
  v_existing boolean;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is distinct from 'administrator'::public.app_role then
    raise exception 'Somente administradores gerenciam devoluções nesta etapa.' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_id is null or p_request_id is null or p_input is null or jsonb_typeof(p_input) <> 'object' then
    raise exception 'Identificação e dados obrigatórios.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  -- Serializa inclusive a criação do registro; o identificador vem do cliente.
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_id::text, 0));
  select * into v_event from public.warranty_return_events where request_id = p_request_id;
  if found then
    if v_event.return_id <> p_id or v_event.performed_by is distinct from auth.uid()
       or (v_event.after_data - array['id','created_by','updated_by','created_at','updated_at']) is distinct from p_input then
      raise exception 'Identificação de envio já utilizada.' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    return v_event.after_data;
  end if;
  select * into v_old from public.warranty_returns where id = p_id for update;
  v_existing := found;
  if v_existing then
    if p_expected_updated_at is null or v_old.updated_at is distinct from p_expected_updated_at then
      raise exception 'Registro alterado. Atualize a lista.' using errcode = '40001';
    end if;
    if v_old.status = 'closed' then
      raise exception 'Registro encerrado não pode ser alterado.' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    v_before := to_jsonb(v_old);
  elsif p_expected_updated_at is not null then
    raise exception 'Registro não encontrado.' using errcode = '40001';
  end if;
  v_new := jsonb_populate_record(null::public.warranty_returns, p_input);
  -- A quantidade é JSON numérico inteiro, evitando arredondar números decimais no cast.
  if jsonb_typeof(p_input->'boxes') is distinct from 'number'
     or (p_input->>'boxes')::numeric <> trunc((p_input->>'boxes')::numeric)
     or v_new.boxes is null or v_new.boxes < 1
     or v_new.product_kind is null or v_new.product_kind not in ('shock','spring','unknown')
     or v_new.ownership is null or v_new.ownership not in ('unknown','ours','other')
     or v_new.status is null or v_new.status not in ('received','awaiting_test','identification','warranty','closed')
     or coalesce(length(trim(v_new.description)),0) not between 1 and 300
     or coalesce(length(trim(v_new.reason)),0) not between 1 and 500
     or coalesce(length(trim(v_new.location)),0) not between 1 and 100
     or v_new.product_code is null or length(v_new.product_code) > 60
     or v_new.destination is null or length(v_new.destination) > 300
     or (v_new.status in ('awaiting_test','identification') and (v_new.product_kind <> 'shock' or v_new.ownership <> 'ours'))
     or (v_new.status = 'warranty' and (v_new.manufacturer_id is null or v_new.product_kind = 'unknown' or v_new.ownership <> 'ours'))
     or (v_new.status = 'closed' and length(trim(v_new.destination)) = 0) then
    raise exception 'Confira os campos e o andamento da devolução.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  -- Já em garantia: identidade/quantidade ficam fixas para manter o vínculo com o saldo.
  if v_existing and v_old.status = 'warranty' and (
    v_new.manufacturer_id is distinct from v_old.manufacturer_id or v_new.product_kind <> v_old.product_kind
    or v_new.boxes <> v_old.boxes or v_new.product_code <> v_old.product_code or v_new.ownership <> v_old.ownership
    or v_new.status not in ('warranty','closed')) then
    raise exception 'Encerre a garantia antes de mudar sua identificação.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_new.status = 'warranty' and (not v_existing or v_old.status <> 'warranty') then
    perform public.change_warranty_boxes(v_new.manufacturer_id, v_new.product_kind, 'in', v_new.boxes,
      'Entrada vinculada à devolução ' || p_id::text, p_request_id);
  elsif v_existing and v_old.status = 'warranty' and v_new.status = 'closed' then
    perform public.change_warranty_boxes(v_old.manufacturer_id, v_old.product_kind, 'out', v_old.boxes,
      'Encerramento da devolução ' || p_id::text || ': ' || v_new.destination, p_request_id);
  end if;
  if v_existing then
    update public.warranty_returns set manufacturer_id=v_new.manufacturer_id, product_kind=v_new.product_kind,
      product_code=v_new.product_code, description=v_new.description, boxes=v_new.boxes, reason=v_new.reason,
      location=v_new.location, ownership=v_new.ownership, status=v_new.status, destination=v_new.destination,
      updated_by=auth.uid(), updated_at=clock_timestamp() where id=p_id returning * into v_new;
  else
    insert into public.warranty_returns (id, manufacturer_id, product_kind, product_code, description, boxes,
      reason, location, ownership, status, destination, created_by, updated_by)
    values (p_id,v_new.manufacturer_id,v_new.product_kind,v_new.product_code,v_new.description,v_new.boxes,
      v_new.reason,v_new.location,v_new.ownership,v_new.status,v_new.destination,auth.uid(),auth.uid()) returning * into v_new;
  end if;
  insert into public.warranty_return_events(request_id,return_id,before_data,after_data,performed_by)
  values(p_request_id,p_id,v_before,to_jsonb(v_new),auth.uid());
  return to_jsonb(v_new);
end;
$$;
revoke all on function public.save_warranty_return(uuid,jsonb,timestamptz,uuid) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.save_warranty_return(uuid,jsonb,timestamptz,uuid) to authenticated;
create or replace function public.inventory_api_version()
returns integer language sql immutable security definer set search_path = '' as $$ select 8 $$;
commit;

begin;
create table public.shock_test_approvals (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  request_id uuid not null unique,
  product_id uuid not null references public.products(id),
  product_code text not null,
  location_id uuid not null references public.locations(id),
  location_code text not null,
  boxes integer not null check (boxes > 0),
  performed_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  actor_display_name text not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp()
);
create index shock_test_approvals_product_date on public.shock_test_approvals(product_code, created_at desc, id desc);
alter table public.shock_test_approvals enable row level security;
revoke all on public.shock_test_approvals from public, anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.shock_test_approvals to authenticated;
create policy shock_test_approvals_read_active on public.shock_test_approvals for select to authenticated
  using ((select private.current_user_role()) is not null);

create function public.approve_shock_boxes(p_product_code text, p_location_code text, p_boxes integer, p_request_id uuid)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_product_id uuid;
  v_location_id uuid;
  v_balance public.stock_balances%rowtype;
  v_event public.shock_test_approvals%rowtype;
  v_name text;
  v_code text := upper(trim(p_product_code));
  v_location text := upper(trim(p_location_code));
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is null or private.current_user_role() not in
    ('administrator'::public.app_role, 'tester'::public.app_role) then
    raise exception 'Somente Administrador ou Testador registra aprovação.' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_boxes is null or p_boxes < 1 or p_request_id is null or coalesce(v_code,'') = '' or coalesce(v_location,'') = '' then
    raise exception 'Informe produto, local e quantidade inteira positiva.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if split_part(v_code,'-',1) in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR') then
    raise exception 'Molas não passam por testagem.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  -- O bloqueio do envio garante que duas tentativas simultâneas não aprovem duas vezes.
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_request_id::text,0));
  select * into v_event from public.shock_test_approvals where request_id=p_request_id;
  if found then
    if v_event.product_code <> v_code or v_event.location_code <> v_location or v_event.boxes <> p_boxes
       or v_event.performed_by is distinct from auth.uid() then
      raise exception 'Este envio já foi usado para outra aprovação.' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    return to_jsonb(v_event);
  end if;
  select id into v_product_id from public.products where code=v_code;
  select id into v_location_id from public.locations where code=v_location and is_active;
  if v_product_id is null or v_location_id is null then
    raise exception 'Produto ou localização não encontrado.' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  select trim(display_name) into v_name from public.profiles where id=auth.uid() and is_active;
  if coalesce(v_name,'') = '' then
    raise exception 'Preencha seu nome em Meu perfil antes de registrar o teste.' using errcode = 'P0003';
  end if;
  select * into v_balance from public.stock_balances
    where product_id=v_product_id and location_id=v_location_id and status_code='awaiting_test' and unit_type='box'
    for update;
  if not found or v_balance.quantity < p_boxes then
    raise exception 'Saldo aguardando teste insuficiente. Atualize o estoque.' using errcode = 'P0001';
  end if;
  -- A quantidade total se conserva: sai da espera e entra como liberada no mesmo local.
  if v_balance.quantity=p_boxes then
    delete from public.stock_balances where id=v_balance.id;
  else
    update public.stock_balances set quantity=quantity-p_boxes, updated_at=clock_timestamp() where id=v_balance.id;
  end if;
  insert into public.stock_balances(product_id,location_id,status_code,unit_type,quantity)
  values(v_product_id,v_location_id,'released_for_sale','box',p_boxes)
  on conflict(product_id,location_id,status_code,unit_type)
  do update set quantity=public.stock_balances.quantity+excluded.quantity, updated_at=clock_timestamp();
  insert into public.shock_test_approvals(request_id,product_id,product_code,location_id,location_code,boxes,performed_by,actor_display_name)
  values(p_request_id,v_product_id,v_code,v_location_id,v_location,p_boxes,auth.uid(),v_name) returning * into v_event;
  insert into public.stock_movements(product_id,movement_type,unit_type,quantity,status_before,status_after,to_location_id,performed_by,notes)
  values(v_product_id,'testing','box',p_boxes,'awaiting_test','released_for_sale',v_location_id,auth.uid(),
    'Aprovação de teste: ' || v_event.id::text || '. Responsável: ' || v_name);
  return to_jsonb(v_event);
end;
$$;
revoke all on function public.approve_shock_boxes(text,text,integer,uuid) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.approve_shock_boxes(text,text,integer,uuid) to authenticated;
create or replace function public.inventory_api_version()
returns integer language sql immutable security definer set search_path = '' as $$ select 9 $$;
commit;

-- Aplicar uma vez depois de shock_testing (API 9).
-- Fornecedor e lote documentam o recebimento; ainda não calculam saldo remanescente por lote.
begin;
create table public.suppliers (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  name text not null check (length(trim(name)) between 1 and 100),
  created_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp()
);
create unique index suppliers_unique_name on public.suppliers(lower(name));
alter table public.suppliers enable row level security;
revoke all on public.suppliers from public,anon,authenticated;
grant select on public.suppliers to authenticated;
create policy suppliers_read_active on public.suppliers for select to authenticated
  using ((select private.current_user_role()) is not null);
create function public.create_supplier(p_name text)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v_name text; v_row public.suppliers%rowtype;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is distinct from 'administrator'::public.app_role then
    raise exception 'Somente administradores cadastram fornecedores.' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  v_name := regexp_replace(trim(coalesce(p_name,'')), '[[:space:]]+', ' ', 'g');
  if length(v_name) not between 1 and 100 then
    raise exception 'Informe um nome entre 1 e 100 caracteres.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  -- Cadastrar novamente o mesmo nome retorna a referência já existente.
  insert into public.suppliers(name,created_by) values(v_name,auth.uid())
  on conflict (lower(name)) do nothing returning * into v_row;
  if v_row.id is null then select * into v_row from public.suppliers where lower(name)=lower(v_name); end if;
  return to_jsonb(v_row);
end; $$;
revoke all on function public.create_supplier(text) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.create_supplier(text) to authenticated;

alter table public.stock_movements
  add column supplier_id uuid references public.suppliers(id),
  add column supplier_name text check (length(supplier_name) <= 100),
  add column lot_reference text check (length(lot_reference) <= 100),
  add column receipt_request_id uuid unique,
  add column receipt_payload jsonb;

create function public.receive_stock_with_supplier(
  p_code text, p_application text, p_location text, p_boxes integer, p_status text,
  p_supplier_id uuid, p_lot_reference text, p_request_id uuid
)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_role public.app_role := private.current_user_role();
  v_code text := upper(trim(p_code));
  v_location text := upper(trim(p_location));
  v_application text := nullif(trim(coalesce(p_application,'')),'');
  v_lot text := nullif(trim(coalesce(p_lot_reference,'')),'');
  v_status text;
  v_supplier_name text;
  v_product uuid;
  v_location_id uuid;
  v_payload jsonb;
  v_movement public.stock_movements%rowtype;
begin
  if v_role is null or v_role not in ('administrator'::public.app_role,'receiving'::public.app_role) then
    raise exception 'Sem permissão para receber estoque.' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  if v_code is null or v_code !~ '^[A-Z]{3}-(100|[1-9][0-9]?)$' or p_boxes is null or p_boxes < 1
     or p_request_id is null or coalesce(v_location,'')='' or length(v_lot)>100 then
    raise exception 'Confira código, local, caixas e lote.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  v_status := case when split_part(v_code,'-',1) in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR') then 'in_stock' else p_status end;
  if v_status is null or v_status not in ('awaiting_test','released_for_sale','in_stock')
     or (split_part(v_code,'-',1) not in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR') and v_status='in_stock') then
    raise exception 'Situação inválida para o produto.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  if v_role='receiving'::public.app_role and v_status='released_for_sale' then
    raise exception 'Recebimento de amortecedores começa aguardando teste.' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  if v_lot is not null and p_supplier_id is null then
    raise exception 'Selecione o fornecedor para informar o lote.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  v_payload := jsonb_build_object('code',v_code,'application',v_application,'location',v_location,'boxes',p_boxes,
    'status',v_status,'supplier_id',p_supplier_id,'lot_reference',v_lot);
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_request_id::text,0));
  select * into v_movement from public.stock_movements where receipt_request_id=p_request_id;
  if found then
    if v_movement.receipt_payload is distinct from v_payload or v_movement.performed_by is distinct from auth.uid() then
      raise exception 'Envio já utilizado para outra entrada.' using errcode='22023';
    end if;
    return to_jsonb(v_movement);
  end if;
  if p_supplier_id is not null then
    select name into v_supplier_name from public.suppliers where id=p_supplier_id;
    if not found then raise exception 'Fornecedor não encontrado.' using errcode='P0002'; end if;
  end if;
  select id into v_location_id from public.locations where code=v_location and is_active;
  if v_location_id is null then raise exception 'Local inexistente ou inativo.' using errcode='P0002'; end if;
  select id into v_product from public.products where code=v_code;
  if v_product is null then
    if v_role <> 'administrator'::public.app_role then
      raise exception 'Somente administradores cadastram produto novo.' using errcode='42501';
    end if;
    if v_application is null or length(v_application)>500 then
      raise exception 'Informe a aplicação do produto novo.' using errcode='22023';
    end if;
    insert into public.products(code,application,default_location_id) values(v_code,v_application,v_location_id)
      on conflict(code) do nothing returning id into v_product;
    if v_product is null then select id into v_product from public.products where code=v_code; end if;
  end if;
  insert into public.stock_balances(product_id,location_id,status_code,unit_type,quantity)
  values(v_product,v_location_id,v_status,'box',p_boxes)
  on conflict(product_id,location_id,status_code,unit_type)
  do update set quantity=public.stock_balances.quantity+excluded.quantity, updated_at=clock_timestamp();
  insert into public.stock_movements(product_id,movement_type,unit_type,quantity,status_after,to_location_id,performed_by,
    supplier_id,supplier_name,lot_reference,receipt_request_id,receipt_payload)
  values(v_product,'receipt','box',p_boxes,v_status,v_location_id,auth.uid(),p_supplier_id,v_supplier_name,v_lot,p_request_id,v_payload)
  returning * into v_movement;
  return to_jsonb(v_movement);
end; $$;
revoke all on function public.receive_stock_with_supplier(text,text,text,integer,text,uuid,text,uuid) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.receive_stock_with_supplier(text,text,text,integer,text,uuid,text,uuid) to authenticated;
create or replace function public.inventory_api_version()
returns integer language sql immutable security definer set search_path='' as $$ select 10 $$;

-- Aplicar uma vez depois de receipt_suppliers (API 10).
-- Primeira versão: somente Administrador registra saídas do estoque normal.
begin;

  add column outflow_reference text check (length(outflow_reference)<=100),
  add column outflow_actor_name text,
  add column outflow_payload jsonb;

create function public.register_stock_outflow(
  p_code text, p_location text, p_status text, p_boxes integer,
  p_reason text, p_notes text, p_reference text, p_request_id uuid
)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path='' as $$
declare
  v_code text := upper(trim(p_code));
  v_location text := upper(trim(p_location));
  v_notes text := nullif(trim(coalesce(p_notes,'')),'');
  v_reference text := nullif(trim(coalesce(p_reference,'')),'');
  v_product uuid;
  v_location_id uuid;
  v_spring boolean;
  v_name text;
  v_payload jsonb;
  v_balance public.stock_balances%rowtype;
  v_movement public.stock_movements%rowtype;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is distinct from 'administrator'::public.app_role then
    raise exception 'Somente administradores registram saídas nesta etapa.' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  if coalesce(v_code,'')='' or coalesce(v_location,'')='' or p_boxes is null or p_boxes<1 or p_request_id is null
    or p_reason is null or p_reason not in ('sale','discard','supplier_return')
    or length(v_notes)>500 or length(v_reference)>100 or (p_reason<>'sale' and v_notes is null) then
    raise exception 'Confira produto, local, caixas, motivo e observação.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  v_spring := split_part(v_code,'-',1) in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR');
  if p_status is null or (v_spring and p_status<>'in_stock')
    or (not v_spring and p_status not in ('awaiting_test','released_for_sale'))
    or (p_reason='sale' and not v_spring and p_status<>'released_for_sale') then
    raise exception 'Venda de amortecedor exige caixas liberadas; molas usam Em estoque.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  v_payload := jsonb_build_object('code',v_code,'location',v_location,'status',p_status,'boxes',p_boxes,
    'reason',p_reason,'notes',v_notes,'reference',v_reference);
  -- Um mesmo envio repetido não retira duas vezes, inclusive se o saldo já zerou.
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_request_id::text,0));
  select * into v_movement from public.stock_movements where outflow_request_id=p_request_id;
  if found then
    if v_movement.outflow_payload is distinct from v_payload or v_movement.performed_by is distinct from auth.uid() then
      raise exception 'Envio já utilizado para outra saída.' using errcode='22023';
    end if;
    return to_jsonb(v_movement);
  end if;
  select id into v_product from public.products where code=v_code;
  select id into v_location_id from public.locations where code=v_location and is_active;
  if v_product is null or v_location_id is null then
    raise exception 'Produto ou localização inexistente/inativa.' using errcode='P0002';
  end if;
  select trim(display_name) into v_name from public.profiles where id=auth.uid() and is_active;
  if coalesce(v_name,'')='' then raise exception 'Preencha seu nome em Meu perfil.' using errcode='P0003'; end if;
  select * into v_balance from public.stock_balances
    where product_id=v_product and location_id=v_location_id and status_code=p_status and unit_type='box' for update;
  if not found or v_balance.quantity<p_boxes then
    raise exception 'Saldo insuficiente. Atualize a consulta.' using errcode='P0001';
  end if;
  if v_balance.quantity=p_boxes then
    delete from public.stock_balances where id=v_balance.id;
  else
    update public.stock_balances set quantity=quantity-p_boxes,updated_at=clock_timestamp() where id=v_balance.id;
  end if;
  insert into public.stock_movements(product_id,movement_type,unit_type,quantity,status_before,from_location_id,
    performed_by,notes,outflow_request_id,outflow_reference,outflow_actor_name,outflow_payload)
  values(v_product,p_reason,'box',p_boxes,p_status,v_location_id,auth.uid(),v_notes,p_request_id,v_reference,v_name,v_payload)
  returning * into v_movement;
  return to_jsonb(v_movement);
end; $$;
revoke all on function public.register_stock_outflow(text,text,text,integer,text,text,text,uuid) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.register_stock_outflow(text,text,text,integer,text,text,text,uuid) to authenticated;

-- Aplicar uma vez depois de stock_outflows (API 11).
-- Limite em caixas por código; null = não configurado, zero = limite configurado em zero.
begin;
alter table public.products
  add column minimum_boxes integer check (minimum_boxes>=0),
  add column minimum_updated_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp();
create table public.product_minimum_events (
  request_id uuid primary key,
  product_id uuid not null references public.products(id),
  product_code text not null,
  minimum_before integer,
  minimum_after integer,
  performed_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,

create function public.set_product_minimum(p_code text,p_minimum integer,p_expected_updated_at timestamptz,p_request_id uuid)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path='' as $$
declare
  v_code text:=upper(trim(p_code));
  v_product public.products%rowtype;
  v_event public.product_minimum_events%rowtype;
  v_before integer;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is distinct from 'administrator'::public.app_role then
    raise exception 'Somente Administrador altera limites.' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  if coalesce(v_code,'')='' or p_minimum<0 or p_request_id is null then
    raise exception 'Informe um limite inteiro não negativo ou vazio.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_request_id::text,0));
  select * into v_event from public.product_minimum_events where request_id=p_request_id;
  if found then
    if v_event.product_code<>v_code or v_event.minimum_after is distinct from p_minimum
      or v_event.performed_by is distinct from auth.uid() then
      raise exception 'Envio já usado para outra alteração.' using errcode='22023';
    end if;
    select * into v_product from public.products where id=v_event.product_id;
    return jsonb_build_object('code',v_product.code,'minimum_boxes',v_product.minimum_boxes,'minimum_updated_at',v_product.minimum_updated_at);
  end if;
  select * into v_product from public.products where code=v_code for update;
  if not found then raise exception 'Produto não encontrado.' using errcode='P0002'; end if;
  if p_expected_updated_at is null or v_product.minimum_updated_at is distinct from p_expected_updated_at then
    raise exception 'Limite alterado. Atualize antes de editar.' using errcode='40001';
  end if;
  if v_product.minimum_boxes is distinct from p_minimum then
    v_before:=v_product.minimum_boxes;
    update public.products set minimum_boxes=p_minimum,minimum_updated_at=clock_timestamp()
      where id=v_product.id returning * into v_product;
    insert into public.product_minimum_events(request_id,product_id,product_code,minimum_before,minimum_after,performed_by,actor_display_name)
      values(p_request_id,v_product.id,v_product.code,v_before,p_minimum,auth.uid(),(select display_name from public.profiles where id=auth.uid()));
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object('code',v_product.code,'minimum_boxes',v_product.minimum_boxes,'minimum_updated_at',v_product.minimum_updated_at);
end; $$;
revoke all on function public.set_product_minimum(text,integer,timestamptz,uuid) from public,anon;

54 linhas · SHA-256 84d149745196e400

-- Aplicar uma vez depois de stock_minimums (API 12).
-- Relatório somente de leitura: vendas não incluem transferência, descarte ou garantia.
begin;
create index stock_movements_dashboard_recent on public.stock_movements(movement_type,created_at desc,id desc)
  where unit_type='box' and movement_type in ('sale','receipt');
create function public.inventory_dashboard(p_category text default 'all')
returns jsonb language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path='' as $$
declare
  v_now timestamptz := statement_timestamp();
  v_since timestamptz;
  v_result jsonb;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is null then
    raise exception 'Conta ativa necessária para consultar o painel.' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  if p_category is null or p_category not in ('all','shocks','springs') then
    raise exception 'Categoria inválida.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  v_since:=v_now-interval '30 days';
  with category_products as (
    select product.id,product.code from public.products product
    where p_category='all'
        or (p_category='springs' and split_part(product.code,'-',1) in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR'))
        or (p_category='shocks' and split_part(product.code,'-',1) not in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR'))
  ), sales as (
    select movement.quantity,product.code from public.stock_movements movement
    join category_products product on product.id=movement.product_id
    where movement.unit_type='box' and movement.movement_type='sale'
      and movement.created_at>=v_since and movement.created_at<=v_now
  ), ranked as (
    select code,sum(quantity) as boxes from sales group by code order by boxes desc,code limit 10
  ), recent as (
    select movement.*,product.code,location.code as location_code from public.stock_movements movement
    join category_products product on product.id=movement.product_id
    left join public.locations location on location.id=movement.to_location_id
    where movement.unit_type='box' and movement.movement_type='receipt' and movement.created_at<=v_now
    order by movement.created_at desc,movement.id desc limit 10
  )
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'as_of',v_now,'period_start',v_since,
    'sold_boxes',coalesce((select sum(quantity) from sales),0),
    'sales_count',(select count(*) from sales),
    'sales_by_product',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('code',code,'boxes',boxes) order by boxes desc,code) from ranked),'[]'::jsonb),
    'receipts',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id',id,'created_at',created_at,'code',code,'boxes',quantity,
      'location',location_code,'supplier',supplier_name,'lot',lot_reference) order by created_at desc,id desc) from recent),'[]'::jsonb)
  ) into v_result;
  return v_result;
end; $$;
revoke all on function public.inventory_dashboard(text) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.inventory_dashboard(text) to authenticated;
create or replace function public.inventory_api_version()
returns integer language sql immutable security definer set search_path='' as $$ select 13 $$;
commit;

values('product-photos','product-photos',false,2097152,array['image/webp'])
on conflict(id) do update set public=false,file_size_limit=2097152,allowed_mime_types=array['image/webp'];

-- Um arquivo por produto: AGM-35.webp ou MGM-35.webp, por exemplo.

using(bucket_id='product-photos' and (select private.current_user_role()) is not null);

with check(bucket_id='product-photos'
  and (select private.current_user_role())='administrator'::public.app_role
  and exists(select 1 from public.products p where name=p.code||'.webp'));

using(bucket_id='product-photos' and (select private.current_user_role())='administrator'::public.app_role)
with check(bucket_id='product-photos'
  and (select private.current_user_role())='administrator'::public.app_role
  and exists(select 1 from public.products p where name=p.code||'.webp'));

Funções para localizar: public.inventory_turnover, public, public.inventory_api_version.

69 linhas · SHA-256 f7fb7b37a45c09b4

-- Giro por CAIXAS VENDIDAS, não por valor financeiro ou saldo médio.
-- Somente leitura; não altera saldos, históricos, contas ou políticas existentes.
begin;
do $$ begin
  if public.inventory_api_version()<13 then
    raise exception 'Aplicar o painel de estoque (API 13) antes dos indicadores de giro.';
  end if;
end $$;
create index if not exists stock_movements_turnover_sales
  on public.stock_movements(product_id,created_at desc)
  where unit_type='box' and movement_type='sale';

create or replace function public.inventory_turnover(p_category text default 'all',p_days integer default 90)
returns jsonb language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path='' as $$
declare
  v_now timestamptz:=statement_timestamp();
  v_since timestamptz;
  v_result jsonb;
begin
  if private.current_user_role() is null then
    raise exception 'Conta ativa necessária para consultar os indicadores.' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  if p_category is null or p_category not in ('all','shocks','springs')
     or p_days is null or p_days not in (30,90,180) then
    raise exception 'Categoria ou período inválido.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  v_since:=v_now-make_interval(days=>p_days);
  with category_products as (
    select p.id,p.code,p.created_at from public.products p
    where p_category='all'
      or (p_category='springs' and split_part(p.code,'-',1) in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR'))
      or (p_category='shocks' and split_part(p.code,'-',1) not in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR'))
  ), sales as (
    select m.product_id,
      coalesce(sum(m.quantity) filter(where m.created_at>=v_since),0) as sold_boxes,
      count(*) filter(where m.created_at>=v_since) as sales_count,
      max(m.created_at) as last_sale_at
    from public.stock_movements m join category_products p on p.id=m.product_id
    where m.unit_type='box' and m.movement_type='sale' and m.created_at<=v_now
    group by m.product_id
  ), balances as (
    select b.product_id,sum(b.quantity) as stock_boxes from public.stock_balances b
    join category_products p on p.id=b.product_id
    where b.unit_type='box' and b.status_code in ('awaiting_test','released_for_sale','in_stock')
    group by b.product_id
  )
  select jsonb_build_object('as_of',v_now,'period_start',v_since,'days',p_days,'category',p_category,
    'products',coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
      'code',p.code,'registered_at',p.created_at,'sold_boxes',coalesce(s.sold_boxes,0),
      'sales_count',coalesce(s.sales_count,0),'last_sale_at',s.last_sale_at,
      'stock_boxes',coalesce(b.stock_boxes,0)) order by p.code),'[]'::jsonb))
  into v_result from category_products p
  left join sales s on s.product_id=p.id left join balances b on b.product_id=p.id;
  return v_result;
end $$;
revoke all on function public.inventory_turnover(text,integer) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.inventory_turnover(text,integer) to authenticated;
-- Reexecutar este arquivo não deve reduzir a versão de uma evolução posterior.
do $$ begin
  if public.inventory_api_version()<14 then
    execute $definition$
      create or replace function public.inventory_api_version()
      returns integer language sql immutable security definer set search_path=''
      as $version$ select 14 $version$
    $definition$;
  end if;
end $$;
commit;

Funções para localizar: public.transfer_stock_once, public, public.inventory_api_version.

65 linhas · SHA-256 5b31b6d2949584b1

-- Transferências recuperáveis: aplicar em homologação após a API 14.
-- Mantém a função anterior para compatibilidade com a publicação anterior.
begin;
do $$ begin
  if public.inventory_api_version() < 14 then
    raise exception 'A migração exige a API 14.';
  end if;
end $$;

create table private.transfer_requests (
  id uuid primary key,
  performed_by uuid not null,
  payload jsonb not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
alter table private.transfer_requests enable row level security;
revoke all on private.transfer_requests from public, anon, authenticated;

create function public.transfer_stock_once(
  p_product_code text, p_from_location_code text, p_to_location_code text,
  p_status_code text, p_quantity integer, p_request_id uuid
) returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path='' as $$
declare
  v_role public.app_role := private.current_user_role();
  v_code text := upper(trim(p_product_code));
  v_from text := upper(trim(p_from_location_code));
  v_to text := upper(trim(p_to_location_code));
  v_payload jsonb;
  v_previous private.transfer_requests%rowtype;
begin
  if v_role is null or v_role not in ('administrator'::public.app_role,'stockkeeper'::public.app_role) then
    raise exception 'Sua função não permite transferências.' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  if p_request_id is null or coalesce(v_code,'')='' or coalesce(v_from,'')='' or coalesce(v_to,'')=''
    or v_from=v_to or p_quantity is null or p_quantity<1 or p_status_code is null
    or p_status_code not in ('awaiting_test','released_for_sale','in_stock') then
    raise exception 'Confira produto, locais, situação e quantidade.' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  v_payload := jsonb_build_object('code',v_code,'from',v_from,'to',v_to,'status',p_status_code,'quantity',p_quantity);
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('transfer-request:'||p_request_id::text,0));
  select * into v_previous from private.transfer_requests where id=p_request_id;
  if found then
    if v_previous.performed_by is distinct from auth.uid() or v_previous.payload is distinct from v_payload then
      raise exception 'Identificador já utilizado para outra transferência.' using errcode='22023';
    end if;
    -- Confirma mesmo após o saldo de origem zerar ou o local ser desativado.
    return jsonb_build_object('id',p_request_id);
  end if;
  -- Serializa as novas transferências do mesmo produto para evitar bloqueios inversos.
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('stock-transfer:'||v_code,0));
  perform public.transfer_stock(v_code,v_from,v_to,p_status_code,p_quantity);
  insert into private.transfer_requests(id,performed_by,payload) values(p_request_id,auth.uid(),v_payload);
  return jsonb_build_object('id',p_request_id);
end $$;
revoke all on function public.transfer_stock_once(text,text,text,text,integer,uuid) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.transfer_stock_once(text,text,text,text,integer,uuid) to authenticated;

do $$ begin
  if public.inventory_api_version() < 15 then
    execute $version$create or replace function public.inventory_api_version()
      returns integer language sql immutable security definer set search_path='' as 'select 15'$version$;
  end if;
end $$;
commit;

-- Acesso da empresa por e-mails autorizados e indicadores reais. Preserva contas atuais.
begin;
create table private.company_allowed_emails (
  email text primary key check (email=lower(trim(email)) and email like '%@%'),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
alter table private.company_allowed_emails enable row level security;
revoke all on private.company_allowed_emails from public,anon,authenticated;
insert into private.company_allowed_emails(email)
 select distinct lower(trim(email)) from auth.users where nullif(trim(email),'') is not null;

create function private.require_company_email() returns trigger
language plpgsql security definer set search_path='' as $$
begin
  if not exists(select 1 from private.company_allowed_emails where email=lower(trim(new.email))) then
    raise exception 'Cadastro restrito a endereços autorizados pela empresa.' using errcode='42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.require_company_email() from public,anon,authenticated;
create trigger require_company_email_before_signup before insert on auth.users
 for each row execute function private.require_company_email();
-- O gatilho existente continua criando profiles INATIVOS e sem cargo administrativo.

create function public.inventory_activity_daily(p_category text default 'all',p_days integer default 30)
returns jsonb language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path='' as $$
declare
 v_today date := (statement_timestamp() at time zone 'America/Bahia')::date;
 v_start timestamptz;
 v_result jsonb;
begin
 if private.current_user_role() is null then
  raise exception 'Conta ativa necessária.' using errcode='42501';
 end if;
 if p_category is null or p_category not in ('all','shocks','springs') or p_days is null or p_days not in (7,30,90) then
  raise exception 'Filtro inválido.' using errcode='22023';
 end if;
 v_start := (v_today-(p_days-1))::timestamp at time zone 'America/Bahia';
 with days as (
  select v_today-(p_days-1)+n as day from generate_series(0,p_days-1) n
 ), activity as (
  select (m.created_at at time zone 'America/Bahia')::date as day,
   sum(m.quantity) filter(where m.movement_type='sale') as sold,
   sum(m.quantity) filter(where m.movement_type='receipt') as received
  from public.stock_movements m join public.products p on p.id=m.product_id
  where m.unit_type='box' and m.movement_type in ('sale','receipt')
   and m.created_at>=v_start and m.created_at<=statement_timestamp()
   and (p_category='all' or (p_category='springs' and split_part(p.code,'-',1) in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR'))
    or (p_category='shocks' and split_part(p.code,'-',1) not in ('MGM','MFI','MRE','MFR')))
  group by 1
 ) select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('day',d.day,'sold_boxes',coalesce(a.sold,0),'received_boxes',coalesce(a.received,0)) order by d.day)
 into v_result from days d left join activity a using(day);
 return v_result;
end $$;
revoke all on function public.inventory_activity_daily(text,integer) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.inventory_activity_daily(text,integer) to authenticated;
do $$ begin
 if public.inventory_api_version()<16 then
  execute $v$create or replace function public.inventory_api_version() returns integer
   language sql immutable security definer set search_path='' as 'select 16'$v$;
 end if;
end $$;
commit;

supabase/tests/operational-outflows.sql

Ensaio isolado: cria dados/respostas de teste para verificar comportamentos. Não deve ser usado como uma migração do banco de produção.

198 linhas · SHA-256 aee555c6d7a1dd35

-- ENSAIO, NÃO MIGRAÇÃO. Todos os dados e alterações de perfil são desfeitos no ROLLBACK.
begin;
set local statement_timeout='45s';
set local lock_timeout='5s';
create temporary table operational_results(test text,passed boolean,detail text) on commit drop;

do $$
declare
  actor uuid;
  actor_name text;
  test_location uuid;
  shock uuid;
  spring uuid;
  shock_code text;
  spring_code text;
  location_code text:='OPTEST-'||upper(gen_random_uuid()::text);
  request_id uuid:=gen_random_uuid();
  first_event jsonb;
  repeated_event jsonb;
  current_event jsonb;
  state text;
  count_changed integer;
  role_under_test public.app_role;
begin
  select id,display_name into actor,actor_name from public.profiles
    where is_active and role='administrator' and nullif(trim(display_name),'') is not null limit 1;
  if actor is null then raise exception 'É necessária uma conta Administrador ativa com nome.'; end if;
  select 'AGM-'||n into shock_code from generate_series(100,1,-1) n
    where not exists(select 1 from public.products p where p.code='AGM-'||n) limit 1;
  select 'MGM-'||n into spring_code from generate_series(100,1,-1) n
    where not exists(select 1 from public.products p where p.code='MGM-'||n) limit 1;
  if shock_code is null or spring_code is null then raise exception 'Sem códigos livres para um ensaio isolado.'; end if;
  insert into public.locations(code) values(location_code) returning id into test_location;
  insert into public.products(code,application,default_location_id)
    values(shock_code,'TESTE TRANSACIONAL - AMORTECEDOR',test_location) returning id into shock;
  insert into public.products(code,application,default_location_id)
    values(spring_code,'TESTE TRANSACIONAL - MOLA',test_location) returning id into spring;
  insert into public.stock_balances(product_id,location_id,status_code,unit_type,quantity) values
    (shock,test_location,'awaiting_test','box',5),
    (shock,test_location,'released_for_sale','box',3),
    (spring,test_location,'in_stock','box',3);
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub',actor::text,true);
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claims',jsonb_build_object('sub',actor,'role','authenticated')::text,true);

  -- A RPC é chamada como authenticated, não com os privilégios do SQL Editor.
  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  first_event:=public.register_stock_outflow(shock_code,location_code,'awaiting_test',1,'discard',
    'TESTE OPERACIONAL TRANSACIONAL - sem descarte físico','OPTEST-DESCARTE',request_id);
  repeated_event:=public.register_stock_outflow(shock_code,location_code,'awaiting_test',1,'discard',
    'TESTE OPERACIONAL TRANSACIONAL - sem descarte físico','OPTEST-DESCARTE',request_id);
  execute 'reset role';
  if (select quantity from public.stock_balances where product_id=shock and location_id=test_location and status_code='awaiting_test')<>4
    then raise exception 'Descarte ou repetição descontou quantidade incorreta.'; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('01_descarte_amortecedor',true,'5 para 4 caixas dentro da transação');
  if first_event->>'id' is distinct from repeated_event->>'id'
    or (select count(*) from public.stock_movements where outflow_request_id=request_id)<>1
    then raise exception 'Reenvio duplicou o evento.'; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('02_reenvio_mesma_requisicao',true,'Mesmo evento; somente uma retirada');
  if first_event->>'movement_type'<>'discard' or (first_event->>'quantity')::int<>1
    or first_event->>'performed_by' is distinct from actor::text
    or first_event->>'outflow_actor_name' is distinct from actor_name
    or first_event->>'created_at' is null or first_event->>'notes' not like 'TESTE OPERACIONAL%'
    or first_event->>'outflow_reference'<>'OPTEST-DESCARTE'
    then raise exception 'Histórico não preservou os dados esperados.'; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('03_historico_e_responsavel',true,'Motivo, caixas, referência, conta, nome e data do servidor');

  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  state:='00000';
  begin
    perform public.register_stock_outflow(shock_code,location_code,'awaiting_test',2,'discard',
      'TESTE OPERACIONAL TRANSACIONAL - sem descarte físico','OPTEST-DESCARTE',request_id);
  exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
  execute 'reset role';
  if state<>'22023' then raise exception 'Requisição reaproveitada com outro conteúdo: SQLSTATE %',state; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('04_reenvio_conteudo_diferente',true,'Mesmo identificador com outra quantidade foi recusado');

  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  current_event:=public.register_stock_outflow(spring_code,location_code,'in_stock',1,'supplier_return',
    'TESTE OPERACIONAL - fornecedor fictício, sem envio físico','OPTEST-DEVOLUCAO',gen_random_uuid());
  execute 'reset role';
  if current_event->>'movement_type'<>'supplier_return'
    or (select quantity from public.stock_balances where product_id=spring and location_id=test_location and status_code='in_stock')<>2
    then raise exception 'Devolução de mola não conservou o saldo esperado.'; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('05_devolucao_mola',true,'3 para 2 caixas, sem testagem');

  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  current_event:=public.register_stock_outflow(shock_code,location_code,'released_for_sale',1,'supplier_return',
    'TESTE OPERACIONAL - fornecedor fictício, sem envio físico','OPTEST-DEVOLUCAO-A',gen_random_uuid());
  execute 'reset role';
  if current_event->>'movement_type'<>'supplier_return'
    or (select quantity from public.stock_balances where product_id=shock and location_id=test_location and status_code='released_for_sale')<>2
    then raise exception 'Devolução de amortecedor não conservou o saldo esperado.'; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('05b_devolucao_amortecedor',true,'3 para 2 caixas liberadas');

  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  current_event:=public.register_stock_outflow(spring_code,location_code,'in_stock',1,'discard',
    'TESTE OPERACIONAL - sem descarte físico','OPTEST-DESCARTE-M',gen_random_uuid());
  execute 'reset role';
  if current_event->>'movement_type'<>'discard'
    or (select quantity from public.stock_balances where product_id=spring and location_id=test_location and status_code='in_stock')<>1
    then raise exception 'Descarte de mola não conservou o saldo esperado.'; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('05c_descarte_mola',true,'2 para 1 caixa, sem testagem');

  -- Restrições conferidas no servidor, sem depender da validação do navegador.
  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  state:='00000';
  begin
    perform public.register_stock_outflow(spring_code,location_code,'in_stock',99,'supplier_return','TESTE','OPTEST',gen_random_uuid());
  exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
  execute 'reset role';
  if state<>'P0001' then raise exception 'Retirada excessiva: SQLSTATE %',state; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('06_saldo_insuficiente',true,'Recusado no banco');

  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  state:='00000';
  begin
    perform public.register_stock_outflow(shock_code,location_code,'awaiting_test',1,'discard',null,'OPTEST',gen_random_uuid());
  exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
  execute 'reset role';
  if state<>'22023' then raise exception 'Descarte sem observação: SQLSTATE %',state; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('07_descarte_sem_motivo',true,'Recusado no banco');

  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  state:='00000';
  begin
    perform public.register_stock_outflow(shock_code,location_code,'awaiting_test',1,'sale','TESTE','OPTEST',gen_random_uuid());
  exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
  execute 'reset role';
  if state<>'22023' then raise exception 'Venda sem teste: SQLSTATE %',state; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('08_venda_aguardando_teste',true,'Amortecedor não liberado foi recusado');

  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  state:='00000';
  begin
    perform public.register_stock_outflow(spring_code,location_code,'in_stock',0,'discard','TESTE','OPTEST',gen_random_uuid());
  exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
  execute 'reset role';
  if state<>'22023' then raise exception 'Quantidade zero: SQLSTATE %',state; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('09_quantidade_zero',true,'Recusada no banco');

  foreach role_under_test in array array['receiving','tester','stockkeeper','viewer']::public.app_role[] loop
    update public.profiles set role=role_under_test where id=actor;
    execute 'set local role authenticated';
    state:='00000';
    begin
      perform public.register_stock_outflow(spring_code,location_code,'in_stock',1,'discard','TESTE','OPTEST',gen_random_uuid());
    exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
    execute 'reset role';
    if state<>'42501' then raise exception 'Cargo % conseguiu registrar saída ou falhou de forma inesperada: %',role_under_test,state; end if;
    insert into operational_results values('10_cargo_'||role_under_test,true,'Saída recusada pela RPC para esta função');
  end loop;

  update public.profiles set role='administrator',is_active=false where id=actor;
  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  select count(*) into count_changed from public.stock_balances;
  state:='00000';
  begin
    perform public.register_stock_outflow(spring_code,location_code,'in_stock',1,'discard','TESTE','OPTEST',gen_random_uuid());
  exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
  execute 'reset role';
  if state<>'42501' or count_changed<>0 then raise exception 'Conta inativa teve acesso indevido.'; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('11_conta_inativa',true,'Sem consulta de saldos nem registro de saída');
  update public.profiles set is_active=true where id=actor;

  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  count_changed:=0;
  begin
    update public.stock_balances set quantity=999 where product_id=spring and location_id=test_location;
    get diagnostics count_changed=row_count;
  exception when insufficient_privilege then count_changed:=0; end;
  execute 'reset role';
  if count_changed<>0 then raise exception 'Escrita direta no saldo foi permitida.'; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('12_escrita_direta_saldo',true,'Sem linhas alteradas fora da RPC');

  execute 'set local role anon';
  state:='00000';
  begin
    perform public.register_stock_outflow(spring_code,location_code,'in_stock',1,'discard','TESTE','OPTEST',gen_random_uuid());
  exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
  execute 'reset role';
  if state<>'42501' then raise exception 'Sessão anônima não foi bloqueada: %',state; end if;
  insert into operational_results values('13_sessao_anonima',true,'Execução da RPC recusada');

  if (select quantity from public.stock_balances where product_id=shock and location_id=test_location and status_code='awaiting_test')<>4
    or (select quantity from public.stock_balances where product_id=spring and location_id=test_location and status_code='in_stock')<>1
    or (select count(*) from public.stock_movements where product_id in(shock,spring))<>4 then
    raise exception 'Tentativa recusada alterou saldos ou gerou histórico.';
  end if;
  insert into operational_results values('14_falhas_sem_efeitos',true,'Somente os quatro eventos válidos existem na transação');
exception when others then
  -- O bloco PL/pgSQL desfaz suas alterações antes de registrar uma falha no relatório temporário.
  execute 'reset role';
  insert into operational_results values('FALHA',false,sqlstate||': '||sqlerrm);
end $$;

select test,passed,detail from operational_results order by test;
rollback;

Ensaio isolado: cria dados/respostas de teste para verificar comportamentos. Não deve ser usado como uma migração do banco de produção.

92 linhas · SHA-256 5a690ae7ebbdd703

-- ENSAIO, NÃO MIGRAÇÃO. Perfis, saldos e eventos são desfeitos ao final.
begin;
set local statement_timeout='45s';
set local lock_timeout='5s';
create temporary table role_test_results(role text,operation text,passed boolean,detail text) on commit drop;
do $$
declare
  actor uuid;
  shock uuid;
  spring uuid;
  source_id uuid;
  target_id uuid;
  shock_code text;
  spring_code text;
  source_code text:='OPROLE-A-'||upper(gen_random_uuid()::text);
  target_code text:='OPROLE-B-'||upper(gen_random_uuid()::text);
  role_under_test public.app_role;
  operation text;
  allowed boolean;
  state text;
  minimum_version timestamptz;
  visible_rows integer;
begin
  select id into actor from public.profiles where is_active and role='administrator' and nullif(trim(display_name),'') is not null limit 1;
  if actor is null then raise exception 'Necessária conta Administrador ativa com nome.'; end if;
  select 'AGM-'||n into shock_code from generate_series(100,1,-1) n where not exists(select 1 from public.products p where p.code='AGM-'||n) limit 1;
  select 'MGM-'||n into spring_code from generate_series(100,1,-1) n where not exists(select 1 from public.products p where p.code='MGM-'||n) limit 1;
  if shock_code is null or spring_code is null then raise exception 'Sem código livre para ensaio.'; end if;
  insert into public.locations(code) values(source_code) returning id into source_id;
  insert into public.locations(code) values(target_code) returning id into target_id;
  insert into public.products(code,application,default_location_id) values(shock_code,'TESTE DE CARGOS - AMORTECEDOR',source_id) returning id into shock;
  insert into public.products(code,application,default_location_id) values(spring_code,'TESTE DE CARGOS - MOLA',source_id) returning id into spring;
  insert into public.stock_balances(product_id,location_id,status_code,unit_type,quantity) values
    (shock,source_id,'awaiting_test','box',20),(shock,source_id,'released_for_sale','box',20),(spring,source_id,'in_stock','box',20);
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub',actor::text,true);
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claims',jsonb_build_object('sub',actor,'role','authenticated')::text,true);
  foreach role_under_test in array array['administrator','receiving','tester','stockkeeper','viewer']::public.app_role[] loop
    update public.profiles set role=role_under_test where id=actor;
    foreach operation in array array['consultar','receber_amortecedor','receber_mola','transferir','aprovar_teste','registrar_saida','alterar_minimo'] loop
      select minimum_updated_at into minimum_version from public.products where id=shock;
      allowed:=case operation
        when 'consultar' then true
        when 'receber_amortecedor' then role_under_test in('administrator','receiving')
        when 'receber_mola' then role_under_test in('administrator','receiving')
        when 'transferir' then role_under_test in('administrator','stockkeeper')
        when 'aprovar_teste' then role_under_test in('administrator','tester')
        else role_under_test='administrator' end;
      execute 'set local role authenticated';
      state:='00000';
      begin
        case operation
          when 'consultar' then
            select count(*) into visible_rows from public.stock_balances where product_id in(shock,spring);
            if visible_rows<3 then raise exception 'Conta ativa não conseguiu consultar os saldos.'; end if;
          when 'receber_amortecedor' then
            perform public.receive_stock_with_supplier(shock_code,null,source_code,1,'awaiting_test',null,null,gen_random_uuid());
          when 'receber_mola' then
            perform public.receive_stock_with_supplier(spring_code,null,source_code,1,'in_stock',null,null,gen_random_uuid());
          when 'transferir' then
            perform public.transfer_stock(shock_code,source_code,target_code,'awaiting_test',1);
          when 'aprovar_teste' then
            perform public.approve_shock_boxes(shock_code,source_code,1,gen_random_uuid());
          when 'registrar_saida' then
            perform public.register_stock_outflow(shock_code,source_code,'awaiting_test',1,'discard','TESTE DE CARGOS - ROLLBACK','OPROLE',gen_random_uuid());
          when 'alterar_minimo' then
            perform public.set_product_minimum(shock_code,2,minimum_version,gen_random_uuid());
        end case;
      exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
      execute 'reset role';
      if (allowed and state<>'00000') or (not allowed and state<>'42501') then
        raise exception 'Cargo %, operação %, esperado permitido=%, SQLSTATE recebido %',role_under_test,operation,allowed,state;
      end if;
      insert into role_test_results values(role_under_test::text,operation,true,case when allowed then 'Permitida pelo banco' else 'Recusada pelo banco (42501)' end);
    end loop;
    if role_under_test in('administrator','tester') then
      execute 'set local role authenticated';
      state:='00000';
      begin
        perform public.approve_shock_boxes(spring_code,source_code,1,gen_random_uuid());
      exception when others then get stacked diagnostics state=returned_sqlstate; end;
      execute 'reset role';
      if state<>'22023' then raise exception 'Mola aceitou testagem ou foi recusada por motivo incorreto: %',state; end if;
      insert into role_test_results values(role_under_test::text,'molas_sem_testagem',true,'Testagem recusada (22023)');
    end if;
  end loop;
exception when others then
  execute 'reset role';
  insert into role_test_results values('FALHA','ensaio',false,sqlstate||': '||sqlerrm);
end $$;
select role,operation,passed,detail from role_test_results order by role,operation;
rollback;
