-- ENSAIO, NÃO MIGRAÇÃO. Fixtures, perfis e histórico são desfeitos no ROLLBACK.
begin;
set local statement_timeout='45s';
set local lock_timeout='5s';
create temporary table turnover_test_results(test text,passed boolean) on commit drop;
do $$
declare
  actor uuid;
  location_id uuid;
  shock uuid;
  spring uuid;
  idle uuid;
  shock_code text;
  spring_code text;
  idle_code text;
  report jsonb;
  row_data jsonb;
  actual_state text;
  period integer;
  role_under_test public.app_role;
  moment timestamptz:=statement_timestamp();
begin
  select id into actor from public.profiles where is_active and role='administrator' limit 1;
  if actor is null then raise exception 'Administrador ativo necessário para o ensaio.'; end if;
  select 'AGM-'||n into shock_code from generate_series(100,1,-1) n where not exists(select 1 from public.products where code='AGM-'||n) limit 1;
  select 'MGM-'||n into spring_code from generate_series(100,1,-1) n where not exists(select 1 from public.products where code='MGM-'||n) limit 1;
  select 'AGM-'||n into idle_code from generate_series(100,1,-1) n where 'AGM-'||n<>shock_code and not exists(select 1 from public.products where code='AGM-'||n) limit 1;
  if shock_code is null or spring_code is null or idle_code is null then raise exception 'Sem códigos livres para fixtures.'; end if;
  insert into public.locations(code) values('TURNOVER-'||upper(gen_random_uuid()::text)) returning id into location_id;
  insert into public.products(code,application,default_location_id) values(shock_code,'ENSAIO TRANSACIONAL - AMORTECEDOR',location_id) returning id into shock;
  insert into public.products(code,application,default_location_id) values(spring_code,'ENSAIO TRANSACIONAL - MOLA',location_id) returning id into spring;
  insert into public.products(code,application,default_location_id) values(idle_code,'ENSAIO TRANSACIONAL - SEM VENDAS',location_id) returning id into idle;
  insert into public.stock_balances(product_id,location_id,status_code,unit_type,quantity) values
    (shock,location_id,'awaiting_test','box',4),(shock,location_id,'released_for_sale','box',2),
    (shock,location_id,'awaiting_test','piece',12),(spring,location_id,'in_stock','box',9),
    (idle,location_id,'awaiting_test','box',7);
  insert into public.stock_movements(product_id,movement_type,unit_type,quantity,created_at) values
    (shock,'sale','box',5,moment-interval '10 days'),
    (shock,'sale','box',3,moment-interval '30 days'),
    (shock,'sale','box',2,moment-interval '31 days'),
    (shock,'sale','box',1,moment-interval '91 days'),
    (shock,'sale','box',777,moment+interval '1 day'),
    (shock,'sale','piece',99,moment-interval '1 day'),
    (shock,'receipt','box',999,moment-interval '1 day'),
    (shock,'transfer','box',80,moment-interval '1 day'),
    (shock,'discard','box',90,moment-interval '1 day'),
    (shock,'supplier_return','box',70,moment-interval '1 day'),
    (spring,'sale','box',4,moment-interval '3 days'),
    (spring,'sale','box',6,moment-interval '100 days');
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub',actor::text,true);
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claims',jsonb_build_object('sub',actor,'role','authenticated')::text,true);
  execute 'set local role authenticated';
  report:=public.inventory_turnover('all',30);
  execute 'reset role';
  select value into row_data from jsonb_array_elements(report->'products') where value->>'code'=shock_code;
  if (row_data->>'sold_boxes')::int<>8 or (row_data->>'sales_count')::int<>2 then raise exception 'Limite de 30 dias ou exclusões incorretos: %',row_data; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('01_limite_exato_30_dias_e_exclusoes',true);
  if (row_data->>'last_sale_at')::timestamptz<>moment-interval '10 days' then raise exception 'Última venda incluiu futuro ou peças.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('02_ultima_venda_sem_futuro_ou_pecas',true);
  if (row_data->>'stock_boxes')::int<>6 then raise exception 'Saldo normal incluiu peças ou deixou aguardando fora.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('03_estoque_shock_inclui_aguardando_sem_pecas',true);
  select value into row_data from jsonb_array_elements(report->'products') where value->>'code'=spring_code;
  if (row_data->>'sold_boxes')::int<>4 or (row_data->>'stock_boxes')::int<>9 then raise exception 'Saldo/venda de mola incorreto.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('04_mola_em_estoque_sem_testagem',true);
  select value into row_data from jsonb_array_elements(report->'products') where value->>'code'=idle_code;
  if row_data is null or (row_data->>'sold_boxes')::int<>0 or row_data->>'last_sale_at' is not null or (row_data->>'stock_boxes')::int<>7 then raise exception 'Produto sem vendas ausente ou incorreto.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('05_catalogo_sem_vendas_preservado',true);
  execute 'set local role authenticated'; report:=public.inventory_turnover('all',90); execute 'reset role';
  select value into row_data from jsonb_array_elements(report->'products') where value->>'code'=shock_code;
  if (row_data->>'sold_boxes')::int<>10 then raise exception 'Período de 90 dias incorreto.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('06_periodo_90_dias',true);
  execute 'set local role authenticated'; report:=public.inventory_turnover('all',180); execute 'reset role';
  select value into row_data from jsonb_array_elements(report->'products') where value->>'code'=shock_code;
  if (row_data->>'sold_boxes')::int<>11 then raise exception 'Período de 180 dias incorreto.'; end if;
  select value into row_data from jsonb_array_elements(report->'products') where value->>'code'=spring_code;
  if (row_data->>'sold_boxes')::int<>10 then raise exception 'Vendas antigas de mola não foram incluídas.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('07_periodo_180_ambas_categorias',true);
  execute 'set local role authenticated'; report:=public.inventory_turnover('shocks',30); execute 'reset role';
  if exists(select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(report->'products') where value->>'code'=spring_code) then raise exception 'Filtro de amortecedor incluiu mola.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('08_filtro_amortecedores',true);
  execute 'set local role authenticated'; report:=public.inventory_turnover('springs',30); execute 'reset role';
  if exists(select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(report->'products') where value->>'code'=shock_code)
    or not exists(select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(report->'products') where value->>'code'=spring_code) then raise exception 'Filtro de molas incorreto.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('09_filtro_molas',true);
  execute 'set local role authenticated'; report:=public.inventory_turnover('all',30); execute 'reset role';
  if (report->>'as_of')::timestamptz<>moment or (report->>'period_start')::timestamptz<>moment-interval '30 days' then raise exception 'Janela temporal incorreta.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('10_janela_temporal_do_servidor',true);
  foreach period in array array[0,31,365,null] loop
    actual_state:='00000';execute 'set local role authenticated';
    begin perform public.inventory_turnover('all',period); exception when others then get stacked diagnostics actual_state=returned_sqlstate; end;
    execute 'reset role';
    if actual_state<>'22023' then raise exception 'Período inválido aceito: %',period; end if;
  end loop;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('11_periodos_invalidos_recusados',true);
  actual_state:='00000';execute 'set local role authenticated';
  begin perform public.inventory_turnover('invalid',30); exception when others then get stacked diagnostics actual_state=returned_sqlstate; end;
  execute 'reset role';if actual_state<>'22023' then raise exception 'Categoria inválida aceita.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('12_categoria_invalida_recusada',true);
  foreach role_under_test in array array['administrator','receiving','tester','stockkeeper','viewer']::public.app_role[] loop
    update public.profiles set role=role_under_test where id=actor;
    execute 'set local role authenticated';report:=public.inventory_turnover('all',30);execute 'reset role';
    if report->'products' is null then raise exception 'Consulta não retornou para %',role_under_test; end if;
    insert into turnover_test_results values('13_acesso_ativo_'||role_under_test,true);
  end loop;
  update public.profiles set role='administrator',is_active=false where id=actor;
  actual_state:='00000';execute 'set local role authenticated';
  begin perform public.inventory_turnover('all',30); exception when others then get stacked diagnostics actual_state=returned_sqlstate; end;
  execute 'reset role';if actual_state<>'42501' then raise exception 'Conta inativa conseguiu consultar.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('14_conta_inativa_recusada',true);
  if has_function_privilege('anon','public.inventory_turnover(text,integer)','execute') then raise exception 'Anon tem acesso ao relatório.'; end if;
  insert into turnover_test_results values('15_sem_execucao_anonima',true);
end $$;
select * from turnover_test_results order by test;
rollback;
scripts/backup-database.ps1

Arquivo de infraestrutura ou ferramenta de apoio. Leia os comentários e consulte a aula sobre pastas para entender onde ele participa do build, dos testes ou da publicação.

Funções para localizar: Invoke.

111 linhas · SHA-256 2afabee532dd1682

# Exportação somente de leitura. A senha nunca é escrita no comando ou no arquivo.
[CmdletBinding()]
param()

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

# Lê stdout e stderr separadamente, inclusive no Windows PowerShell 5.
# Avisos de ferramentas nativas não devem interromper um backup cujo código de saída é zero.
function Invoke-BackupTool {
    param([string]$Executable, [string[]]$ToolArguments, [string]$ErrorLog, [string]$OutputLog)
    $process = New-Object System.Diagnostics.Process
    try {
        $process.StartInfo.FileName = $Executable
        # Os argumentos são gerados pelo script; aspas não são permitidas em nomes de arquivo Windows.
        foreach ($argument in $ToolArguments) { if ($argument.Contains('"')) { throw 'Argumento inválido para exportação.' } }
        $process.StartInfo.Arguments = ($ToolArguments | ForEach-Object { '"' + $_ + '"' }) -join ' '
        $process.StartInfo.UseShellExecute = $false
        $process.StartInfo.CreateNoWindow = $true
        $process.StartInfo.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
        $process.StartInfo.RedirectStandardError = $true
        [void]$process.Start()
        $outputTask = $process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync()
        $errorTask = $process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync()
        $process.WaitForExit()
        $outputText = $outputTask.GetAwaiter().GetResult()
        $errorText = $errorTask.GetAwaiter().GetResult()
        [IO.File]::WriteAllText($ErrorLog, $errorText, [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
        if ($OutputLog) { [IO.File]::WriteAllText($OutputLog, $outputText, [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)) }
        if ($process.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw "Falha em $([IO.Path]::GetFileName($Executable)). Consulte: $ErrorLog" }
    }
    finally { $process.Dispose() }
}
$projectRoot = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$outputsRoot = Split-Path $projectRoot -Parent
$toolsRoot = Join-Path $outputsRoot 'tools'
$dumpTool = Join-Path $toolsRoot 'pgsql/bin/pg_dump.exe'
$rolesTool = Join-Path $toolsRoot 'pgsql/bin/pg_dumpall.exe'
$restoreTool = Join-Path $toolsRoot 'pgsql/bin/pg_restore.exe'
$rootCertificate = Join-Path $toolsRoot 'liber-postgres-ca.pem'
foreach ($requiredFile in @($dumpTool, $rolesTool, $restoreTool, $rootCertificate)) {
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $requiredFile -PathType Leaf)) {
        throw "Ferramenta ou certificado ausente: $requiredFile"
    }
}

# Caminho fora do repositório: o backup pode conter dados privados e contas.
$backupRoot = Join-Path $outputsRoot 'Backups/Supabase'
$backupDirectory = Join-Path $backupRoot ((Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0, 6))
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $backupDirectory -Force | Out-Null
$dumpPath = Join-Path $backupDirectory 'database.dump'
$rolesPath = Join-Path $backupDirectory 'roles.sql'
$manifestPath = Join-Path $backupDirectory 'manifest.json'
$manifest = [ordered]@{
    project = 'tfatagbhmkeelbvstcvb'
    createdAt = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString('o')
    status = 'incomplete'
    restoreTested = $false
    coverage = 'Logical database export and role definitions without role passwords. Storage files and platform settings require separate backup.'
}
$manifest | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content -LiteralPath $manifestPath -Encoding UTF8

# A conexão Session pooler foi conferida no painel do projeto; suporta rede IPv4.
$connectionArgs = @('--host=aws-0-us-east-1.pooler.supabase.com', '--port=5432', '--username=postgres.tfatagbhmkeelbvstcvb', '--dbname=postgres', '--no-password')
$previousEnvironment = @{}
foreach ($variableName in @('PGPASSWORD', 'PGSSLMODE', 'PGSSLROOTCERT', 'PGCONNECT_TIMEOUT', 'PGOPTIONS')) {
    $previousEnvironment[$variableName] = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($variableName, 'Process')
}
$secret = $null
$passwordPointer = [IntPtr]::Zero
try {
    Write-Host 'Backup de leitura do banco LIBER. Não digite a senha do login do site.'
    $secret = Read-Host 'Senha do BANCO do Supabase (entrada oculta)' -AsSecureString
    $passwordPointer = [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::SecureStringToBSTR($secret)
    $env:PGPASSWORD = [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::PtrToStringBSTR($passwordPointer)
    $env:PGSSLMODE = 'verify-full'
    $env:PGSSLROOTCERT = $rootCertificate
    $env:PGCONNECT_TIMEOUT = '20'
    # Impede escrita no banco mesmo se uma configuração local tentar mudá-la.
    $env:PGOPTIONS = '-c default_transaction_read_only=on'

    Write-Host 'Exportando banco...'
    Invoke-BackupTool -Executable $dumpTool -ToolArguments ($connectionArgs + @('--format=custom', '--lock-wait-timeout=15s', "--file=$dumpPath")) -ErrorLog (Join-Path $backupDirectory 'database-errors.log')

    Write-Host 'Exportando definições de cargos, sem senhas...'
    # --database escolhe o banco inicial usado pela ferramenta para consultar os cargos.
    $roleConnectionArgs = @('--host=aws-0-us-east-1.pooler.supabase.com', '--port=5432', '--username=postgres.tfatagbhmkeelbvstcvb', '--database=postgres', '--no-password')
    Invoke-BackupTool -Executable $rolesTool -ToolArguments ($roleConnectionArgs + @('--roles-only', '--no-role-passwords', "--file=$rolesPath")) -ErrorLog (Join-Path $backupDirectory 'roles-errors.log')

    # Lê o índice do arquivo local. Não se conecta a um destino nem restaura dados.
    Invoke-BackupTool -Executable $restoreTool -ToolArguments @('--list', $dumpPath) -OutputLog (Join-Path $backupDirectory 'contents.list') -ErrorLog (Join-Path $backupDirectory 'validation-errors.log')
    $manifest.status = 'exported'
    $manifest['databaseSha256'] = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $dumpPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
    $manifest['rolesSha256'] = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $rolesPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
    $manifest | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content -LiteralPath $manifestPath -Encoding UTF8
    Write-Host 'Exportação concluída e índice legível. A restauração em ambiente separado ainda precisa ser testada.'
}
catch {
    $manifest.status = 'failed'
    $manifest | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content -LiteralPath $manifestPath -Encoding UTF8
    throw
}
finally {
    if ($passwordPointer -ne [IntPtr]::Zero) { [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ZeroFreeBSTR($passwordPointer) }
    if ($null -ne $secret) { $secret.Dispose() }
    foreach ($variableName in $previousEnvironment.Keys) {
        [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable($variableName, $previousEnvironment[$variableName], 'Process')
    }
    Write-Host "Pasta privada do backup: $backupDirectory"
    Write-Host 'Não envie os arquivos de backup ao GitHub nem ao chat.'
}

# Testes locais: não solicitam senha e não conectam ao Supabase.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$tokens = $null
$parseErrors = $null
$sourcePath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'backup-database.ps1'
$syntax = [Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile($sourcePath, [ref]$tokens, [ref]$parseErrors)
if ($parseErrors.Count) { throw ($parseErrors.Message -join '; ') }
$function = $syntax.Find({ param($node) $node -is [Management.Automation.Language.FunctionDefinitionAst] -and $node.Name -eq 'Invoke-BackupTool' }, $true)
Invoke-Expression $function.Extent.Text

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $testDirectory | Out-Null

$warningLog = Join-Path $testDirectory 'warning.log'
Invoke-BackupTool -Executable $nodeExecutable -ToolArguments @('-e', "process.stderr.write('aviso');") -ErrorLog $warningLog
if ((Get-Content -Raw -LiteralPath $warningLog) -ne 'aviso') { throw 'Aviso não preservado.' }
$failed = $false
try { Invoke-BackupTool -Executable $nodeExecutable -ToolArguments @('-e', 'process.exit(7)') -ErrorLog (Join-Path $testDirectory 'error.log') }
catch { $failed = $true }
if (-not $failed) { throw 'Código de saída não zero deveria falhar.' }

$toolsRoot = Join-Path (Split-Path (Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent) -Parent) 'tools/pgsql/bin'
foreach ($item in @(@('connectionArgs', 'pg_dump.exe'), @('roleConnectionArgs', 'pg_dumpall.exe'))) {
    $name = $item[0]
    $assignment = $syntax.Find({ param($node) $node -is [Management.Automation.Language.AssignmentStatementAst] -and $node.Left.Extent.Text -eq ('$' + $name) }.GetNewClosure(), $true)
    Invoke-Expression $assignment.Extent.Text
    $arguments = @(Get-Variable -Name $name -ValueOnly | ForEach-Object {
        if ($_ -like '--host=*') { '--host=127.0.0.1' }
        elseif ($_ -like '--port=*') { '--port=1' }
        else { $_ }
    })
    $log = Join-Path $testDirectory ($item[1] + '.log')
    # A porta local fechada impede qualquer exportação, mas permite validar o parser real.
    try { Invoke-BackupTool -Executable (Join-Path $toolsRoot $item[1]) -ToolArguments $arguments -ErrorLog $log }
    catch { }
    $message = Get-Content -Raw -LiteralPath $log
    if ($message -match 'illegal option|unrecognized option|invalid option' -or $message -notmatch 'connection to server') { throw "Parâmetros rejeitados por $($item[1]): $message" }
}
Write-Output 'OK: sintaxe, aviso com saída zero, falha com saída não zero e parâmetros aceitos pelas duas ferramentas. Nenhuma senha usada ou conexão ao Supabase realizada.'

# Disputa com COMMIT em tabelas isoladas; não registra retiradas no estoque público.
# Copia a função atual, executa dois clientes e remove apenas o schema criado por este ensaio.
[CmdletBinding()]
param()
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$projectRoot=Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$outputsRoot=Split-Path $projectRoot -Parent
$psql=Join-Path $outputsRoot 'tools/pgsql/bin/psql.exe'
$certificate=Join-Path $outputsRoot 'tools/liber-postgres-ca.pem'
if(!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $psql) -or !(Test-Path -LiteralPath $certificate)){throw 'Ferramentas PostgreSQL ou certificado ausentes.'}
$reportDirectory=Join-Path $outputsRoot ('verifications/committed-concurrency-'+(Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss')+'-'+[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0,6))
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $reportDirectory -Force | Out-Null
$workers=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
$previousEnvironment=@{}
foreach($name in @('PGPASSWORD','PGSSLMODE','PGSSLROOTCERT','PGCONNECT_TIMEOUT','PGOPTIONS')){$previousEnvironment[$name]=[Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($name,'Process')}
$secret=$null
$pointer=[IntPtr]::Zero
$report=[ordered]@{status='incomplete';createdAt=[DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.ToString('o');permanentWrites=$false;restorePerformed=$false}

function SqlLiteral([string]$value){return "'"+$value.Replace("'","''")+"'"}
function Start-PgWorker([string]$name,[string]$sql){
    $sqlFile=Join-Path $reportDirectory ($name+'.sql')
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($sqlFile,$sql,[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    $arguments=@('--host=aws-0-us-east-1.pooler.supabase.com','--port=5432','--username=postgres.tfatagbhmkeelbvstcvb','--dbname=postgres','--no-password','--no-psqlrc','--tuples-only','--no-align','--set=ON_ERROR_STOP=1',"--file=$sqlFile")
    $process=New-Object Diagnostics.Process
    $process.StartInfo.FileName=$psql
    $process.StartInfo.Arguments=($arguments|ForEach-Object{'"'+$_+'"'}) -join ' '
    $process.StartInfo.UseShellExecute=$false
    $process.StartInfo.CreateNoWindow=$true
    $process.StartInfo.RedirectStandardOutput=$true
    $process.StartInfo.RedirectStandardError=$true
    [void]$process.Start()
    $worker=[PSCustomObject]@{Name=$name;Process=$process;Output=$process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();Error=$process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync()}
    [void]$workers.Add($worker)
    return $worker
}
function Complete-PgWorker($worker){
    if(!$worker.Process.WaitForExit(35000)){throw "Tempo excedido em $($worker.Name)."}
    $output=$worker.Output.GetAwaiter().GetResult()
    $errors=$worker.Error.GetAwaiter().GetResult()
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $reportDirectory ($worker.Name+'.out')),$output,[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $reportDirectory ($worker.Name+'.log')),$errors,[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    if($worker.Process.ExitCode -ne 0){throw "Ensaio $($worker.Name) falhou. Consulte o arquivo .log da pasta do relatório."}
    return $output
}

$snapshotSql=@'
select jsonb_build_object(
 'products',(select count(*) from public.products),
 'movements',(select count(*) from public.stock_movements),
 'boxes',(select sum(quantity) from public.stock_balances where unit_type='box' and status_code in('awaiting_test','released_for_sale','in_stock')),
 'balances_hash',(select md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(b)::text,'' order by id),'')) from public.stock_balances b),
 'movements_hash',(select md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(m)::text,'' order by id),'')) from public.stock_movements m),
 'profiles_hash',(select md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(p)::text,'' order by id),'')) from public.profiles p)
);
'@
# O nome é gerado aqui; nenhum nome de schema é recebido do usuário.
$testSchema='liber_test_'+[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N')
$report['scope']='Committed race using isolated table/function copies, not the public RPC endpoint.'
$report['testSchema']=$testSchema
$schemaCreated=$false
try{
    Write-Host 'Teste NOVO: primeira retirada confirmada; segunda deve ser recusada.'
    Write-Host 'Usa tabelas isoladas; nenhum saldo público será retirado.'
    $secret=Read-Host 'Senha do BANCO Supabase (oculta)' -AsSecureString
    $pointer=[Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::SecureStringToBSTR($secret)
    $env:PGPASSWORD=[Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::PtrToStringBSTR($pointer)
    $env:PGSSLMODE='verify-full'
    $env:PGSSLROOTCERT=$certificate
    $env:PGCONNECT_TIMEOUT='15'
    $env:PGOPTIONS='-c statement_timeout=20000 -c idle_in_transaction_session_timeout=20000'
    $baseline=(Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker 'baseline' $snapshotSql)).Trim()|ConvertFrom-Json
    $setupSql=@"
begin;
create schema $testSchema;
revoke all on schema $testSchema from public;
create table $testSchema.stock_balances (like public.stock_balances including all);
create table $testSchema.stock_movements (like public.stock_movements including all);
-- LIKE não copia triggers/FKs. Não compartilhar defaults de sequências.
do `$setup`$ declare definition text; begin
 if exists(select 1 from pg_attrdef d where d.adrelid in
  ('$testSchema.stock_balances'::regclass,'$testSchema.stock_movements'::regclass)
  and pg_get_expr(d.adbin,d.adrelid) like '%nextval%') then
  raise exception 'Ensaio interrompido: default de sequência compartilhada.';
 end if;
 definition:=pg_get_functiondef('public.register_stock_outflow(text,text,text,integer,text,text,text,uuid)'::regprocedure);
 definition:=replace(definition,'public.register_stock_outflow(', '$testSchema.register_stock_outflow(');
 definition:=replace(definition,'public.stock_balances','$testSchema.stock_balances');
 definition:=replace(definition,'public.stock_movements','$testSchema.stock_movements');
 if position('public.stock_balances' in definition)>0 or position('public.stock_movements' in definition)>0
  or position('$testSchema.register_stock_outflow(' in definition)=0 then
  raise exception 'Não foi possível isolar a função.';
 end if;
 execute definition;
end `$setup`$;
revoke all on function $testSchema.register_stock_outflow(text,text,text,integer,text,text,text,uuid) from public,anon;
grant usage on schema $testSchema to authenticated;
grant execute on function $testSchema.register_stock_outflow(text,text,text,integer,text,text,text,uuid) to authenticated;
commit;
"@
    # Tentar limpeza mesmo se a confirmação do setup se perder após o COMMIT.
    $schemaCreated=$true
    [void](Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker 'setup' $setupSql))
    $checks=@()
    foreach($category in @('shocks','springs')){
        $springCondition=if($category -eq 'springs'){"p.code ~ '^(MGM|MFI|MRE|MFR)-'"}else{"p.code ~ '^(AGM|AFI|ARE|AFR)-'"}
        $targetSql=@"
select jsonb_build_object('actor',(select id from public.profiles where is_active and role='administrator' and nullif(trim(display_name),'') is not null limit 1),
 'product',p.id,'code',p.code,'location',l.code,'location_id',l.id,
 'status',case when '$category'='springs' then 'in_stock' else 'released_for_sale' end)
from public.products p cross join lateral(select id,code from public.locations where is_active order by code limit 1) l
where $springCondition order by p.code limit 1;
"@
        $target=(Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker ($category+'-target') $targetSql)).Trim()|ConvertFrom-Json
        if(!$target.actor -or !$target.product){throw "Sem conta/cadastro para testar $category."}
        $actor=([guid]$target.actor).ToString()
        $product=([guid]$target.product).ToString()
        $locationId=([guid]$target.location_id).ToString()
        $code=SqlLiteral $target.code
        $location=SqlLiteral $target.location
        $status=SqlLiteral $target.status
        $winnerId=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString()
        $loserId=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString()
        [void](Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker ($category+'-seed') "insert into $testSchema.stock_balances(product_id,location_id,status_code,unit_type,quantity) values('$product','$locationId',$status,'box',1);"))
        $prefix=@"
begin;
select set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub','$actor',true);
select set_config('request.jwt.claims',jsonb_build_object('sub','$actor','role','authenticated')::text,true);
set local role authenticated;
"@
        $readyFile=(Join-Path $reportDirectory ($category+'-ready.txt')).Replace('\','/')
        if($readyFile.Contains("'")){throw 'Caminho não suportado.'}
        $winnerSql=$prefix+@"

select $testSchema.register_stock_outflow($code,$location,$status,1,'discard','ENSAIO ISOLADO','COMMIT-RACE','$winnerId');
\o '$readyFile'
select 'WINNER_LOCKED';
\o
select pg_sleep(6);
commit;
select 'WINNER_COMMITTED';
"@
        Write-Host "Testando $category com duas conexões..."
        $winner=Start-PgWorker ($category+'-winner') $winnerSql
        $watch=[Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
        $ready=$false
        while($watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -lt 15){
            if((Test-Path -LiteralPath $readyFile) -and ((Get-Content -Raw -LiteralPath $readyFile) -match 'WINNER_LOCKED')){$ready=$true;break}
            if($winner.Process.HasExited){break}
            Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 100
        }
        if(!$ready){[void](Complete-PgWorker $winner);throw 'Primeira conexão não confirmou o bloqueio.'}
        # NOWAIT prova que o bloqueio existe quando a segunda conexão entra.
        # Em seguida, ela aguarda a primeira confirmar e precisa receber P0001.
        $loserSql=$prefix+@"

set local lock_timeout='10s';
do `$loser`$ declare actual_state text:='00000'; begin
 begin
  perform 1 from $testSchema.stock_balances where product_id='$product' for update nowait;
 exception when lock_not_available then actual_state:='55P03'; end;
 if actual_state<>'55P03' then raise exception 'A segunda conexão não encontrou o bloqueio esperado.'; end if;
 actual_state:='00000';
 begin
  perform $testSchema.register_stock_outflow($code,$location,$status,1,'discard','ENSAIO ISOLADO','COMMIT-RACE','$loserId');
 exception when others then get stacked diagnostics actual_state=returned_sqlstate; end;
 if actual_state<>'P0001' then raise exception 'Esperado saldo insuficiente após COMMIT; recebido %',actual_state; end if;
end `$loser`$;
rollback;
select 'LOSER_REJECTED_AFTER_COMMIT';
"@
        # O probe NOWAIT precisa apenas ler/bloquear a tabela isolada como authenticated.
        [void](Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker ($category+'-probe-grant') "grant select,update on $testSchema.stock_balances to authenticated;"))
        $loser=Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker ($category+'-loser') $loserSql)
        $winnerOutput=Complete-PgWorker $winner
        if($loser -notmatch 'LOSER_REJECTED_AFTER_COMMIT' -or $winnerOutput -notmatch 'WINNER_COMMITTED'){throw 'Confirmações ausentes.'}
        # Reenvio após COMMIT: mesma requisição retorna o evento já salvo.
        $retrySql=$prefix+@"

select $testSchema.register_stock_outflow($code,$location,$status,1,'discard','ENSAIO ISOLADO','COMMIT-RACE','$winnerId');
commit;
do `$verify`$ begin
 if (select count(*) from $testSchema.stock_movements where product_id='$product')<>1
  or exists(select 1 from $testSchema.stock_balances where product_id='$product')
  or not exists(select 1 from $testSchema.stock_movements where outflow_request_id='$winnerId') then
  raise exception 'Saldo ou número de eventos incorreto.';
 end if;
end `$verify`$;
select 'ONE_EVENT_ZERO_BALANCE';
"@
        $retry=Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker ($category+'-retry') $retrySql)
        if($retry -notmatch 'ONE_EVENT_ZERO_BALANCE'){throw 'Reenvio sem verificação.'}
        $checks+=@("${category}_winner_committed","${category}_loser_rejected_after_commit","${category}_retry_one_event")
    }
    $report['checks']=$checks
    $report.status='checks_passed_cleanup_pending'
}catch{
    $report.status='failed'
    $report['error']=$_.Exception.Message
    throw
}finally{
    foreach($worker in $workers){try{if(!$worker.Process.HasExited){$worker.Process.Kill();$worker.Process.WaitForExit(5000)|Out-Null}}catch{}}
    try{
        if($schemaCreated){
            # Somente o nome UUID gerado por esta execução pode ser removido.
            if($testSchema -notmatch '^liber_test_[a-f0-9]{32}$'){throw 'Nome de schema inválido.'}
            [void](Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker 'cleanup' "drop schema if exists $testSchema cascade;"))
            $report['isolatedSchemaRemoved']=$true
        }
        if($baseline){
            $after=(Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker 'after' $snapshotSql)).Trim()|ConvertFrom-Json
            $report['baseline']=$baseline
            $report['after']=$after
            foreach($field in @('products','movements','boxes','balances_hash','movements_hash','profiles_hash')){
                if($baseline.$field -ne $after.$field){throw "Dados públicos mudaram em $field; revisar atividade externa, sem corrigir automaticamente."}
            }
        }
        if($report.status -eq 'checks_passed_cleanup_pending'){$report.status='passed';Write-Host 'PASSOU: COMMIT, recusa concorrente e reenvio nas duas categorias; estoque público preservado.'}
    }catch{$report.status='failed';$report['cleanupOrVerificationError']=$_.Exception.Message;Write-Warning $_.Exception.Message}
    $report|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6|Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $reportDirectory 'report.json') -Encoding UTF8
    foreach($worker in $workers){$worker.Process.Dispose()}
    if($pointer -ne [IntPtr]::Zero){[Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ZeroFreeBSTR($pointer)}
    if($secret){$secret.Dispose()}
    foreach($name in $previousEnvironment.Keys){[Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable($name,$previousEnvironment[$name],'Process')}
    Write-Host "Relatório privado: $reportDirectory"
}
scripts/check-concurrency.ps1

Regressão automatizada: importa o código real ou uma transpilação e usa assert para recusar resultados inesperados. Onde há mocks, a verificação não comprova a rede/serviço hospedado.

Funções para localizar: SqlLiteral, Start, Complete.

166 linhas · SHA-256 297c7ec3c565f382

# Ensaio com duas conexões REAIS; todas as retiradas terminam em ROLLBACK.
# Não cria contas, não muda cargos e não grava movimentações permanentes.
[CmdletBinding()]
param()
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$projectRoot=Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$outputsRoot=Split-Path $projectRoot -Parent
$psql=Join-Path $outputsRoot 'tools/pgsql/bin/psql.exe'
$certificate=Join-Path $outputsRoot 'tools/liber-postgres-ca.pem'
if(!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $psql) -or !(Test-Path -LiteralPath $certificate)){throw 'Ferramentas PostgreSQL ou certificado ausentes.'}
$reportDirectory=Join-Path $outputsRoot ('verifications/concurrency-'+(Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss')+'-'+[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0,6))
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $reportDirectory -Force | Out-Null
$workers=New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
$previousEnvironment=@{}
foreach($name in @('PGPASSWORD','PGSSLMODE','PGSSLROOTCERT','PGCONNECT_TIMEOUT','PGOPTIONS')){$previousEnvironment[$name]=[Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($name,'Process')}
$secret=$null
$pointer=[IntPtr]::Zero
$report=[ordered]@{status='incomplete';createdAt=[DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.ToString('o');permanentWrites=$false;restorePerformed=$false}

function SqlLiteral([string]$value){return "'"+$value.Replace("'","''")+"'"}
function Start-PgWorker([string]$name,[string]$sql){
    $sqlFile=Join-Path $reportDirectory ($name+'.sql')
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($sqlFile,$sql,[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    $arguments=@('--host=aws-0-us-east-1.pooler.supabase.com','--port=5432','--username=postgres.tfatagbhmkeelbvstcvb','--dbname=postgres','--no-password','--no-psqlrc','--tuples-only','--no-align','--set=ON_ERROR_STOP=1',"--file=$sqlFile")
    $process=New-Object Diagnostics.Process
    $process.StartInfo.FileName=$psql
    $process.StartInfo.Arguments=($arguments|ForEach-Object{'"'+$_+'"'}) -join ' '
    $process.StartInfo.UseShellExecute=$false
    $process.StartInfo.CreateNoWindow=$true
    $process.StartInfo.RedirectStandardOutput=$true
    $process.StartInfo.RedirectStandardError=$true
    [void]$process.Start()
    $worker=[PSCustomObject]@{Name=$name;Process=$process;Output=$process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();Error=$process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync()}
    [void]$workers.Add($worker)
    return $worker
}
function Complete-PgWorker($worker){
    if(!$worker.Process.WaitForExit(35000)){throw "Tempo excedido em $($worker.Name)."}
    $output=$worker.Output.GetAwaiter().GetResult()
    $errors=$worker.Error.GetAwaiter().GetResult()
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $reportDirectory ($worker.Name+'.out')),$output,[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $reportDirectory ($worker.Name+'.log')),$errors,[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    if($worker.Process.ExitCode -ne 0){throw "Ensaio $($worker.Name) falhou. Consulte o arquivo .log da pasta do relatório."}
    return $output
}

$snapshotSql=@'
select jsonb_build_object(
 'products',(select count(*) from public.products),
 'movements',(select count(*) from public.stock_movements),
 'boxes',(select sum(quantity) from public.stock_balances where unit_type='box' and status_code in('awaiting_test','released_for_sale','in_stock')),
 'balances_hash',(select md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(b)::text,'' order by id),'')) from public.stock_balances b),
 'movements_hash',(select md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(m)::text,'' order by id),'')) from public.stock_movements m),
 'profiles_hash',(select md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(p)::text,'' order by id),'')) from public.profiles p)
);
'@
try{
    Write-Host 'Ensaio transacional de concorrência: saldos e históricos serão preservados.'
    $secret=Read-Host 'Senha do BANCO Supabase (oculta)' -AsSecureString
    $pointer=[Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::SecureStringToBSTR($secret)
    $env:PGPASSWORD=[Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::PtrToStringBSTR($pointer)
    $env:PGSSLMODE='verify-full'
    $env:PGSSLROOTCERT=$certificate
    $env:PGCONNECT_TIMEOUT='15'
    $env:PGOPTIONS='-c statement_timeout=20000 -c idle_in_transaction_session_timeout=20000'
    $baseline=(Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker 'baseline' $snapshotSql)).Trim()|ConvertFrom-Json
    $targetSql=@'
select jsonb_build_object('actor',(select id from public.profiles where is_active and role='administrator' and nullif(trim(display_name),'') is not null limit 1),
 'code',p.code,'location',l.code,'status',b.status_code,'boxes',b.quantity)
from public.stock_balances b join public.products p on p.id=b.product_id join public.locations l on l.id=b.location_id
where b.unit_type='box' and b.quantity>0 and l.is_active and b.status_code in('awaiting_test','released_for_sale','in_stock')
order by b.quantity,p.code,l.code limit 1;
'@
    $target=(Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker 'target' $targetSql)).Trim()|ConvertFrom-Json
    if(!$target.actor -or !$target.code){throw 'Sem Administrador ativo ou saldo para o ensaio.'}
    $actor=([guid]$target.actor).ToString()
    $boxes=[int]$target.boxes
    if($boxes -lt 1){throw 'Saldo inválido para o ensaio.'}
    $code=SqlLiteral $target.code
    $location=SqlLiteral $target.location
    $status=SqlLiteral $target.status
    $holderRequest=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString()
    $contenderRequest=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString()
    $prefix=@"
begin;
set local statement_timeout='18s';
select set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub','$actor',true);
select set_config('request.jwt.claims',jsonb_build_object('sub','$actor','role','authenticated')::text,true);
set local role authenticated;
"@
    $readyFile=(Join-Path $reportDirectory 'holder-ready.txt').Replace('\','/')
    if($readyFile.Contains("'")){throw 'O caminho do relatório contém caractere não suportado.'}
    $holderSql=$prefix+@"

set local lock_timeout='2s';
do `$holder`$ begin
 perform public.register_stock_outflow($code,$location,$status,$boxes,'discard','OPTEST-CONCORRENCIA-ROLLBACK','OPTEST-HOLDER','$holderRequest'::uuid);
end `$holder`$;
\o '$readyFile'
select 'HOLDER_LOCKED';
\o
select pg_sleep(8);
rollback;
select 'HOLDER_ROLLED_BACK';
"@
    Write-Host 'Conexão A: reservando o saldo dentro de uma transação...'
    $holder=Start-PgWorker 'holder' $holderSql
    $watch=[Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
    $ready=$false
    while($watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -lt 18){
        if((Test-Path -LiteralPath $readyFile) -and ((Get-Content -Raw -LiteralPath $readyFile) -match 'HOLDER_LOCKED')){$ready=$true;break}
        if($holder.Process.HasExited){break}
        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 100
    }
    if(!$ready){[void](Complete-PgWorker $holder);throw 'Conexão A não confirmou o bloqueio. Nenhum resultado de concorrência será declarado.'}
    $contenderSql=$prefix+@"

set local lock_timeout='1200ms';
do `$contender`$ declare actual_state text:='00000'; begin
 begin
  perform public.register_stock_outflow($code,$location,$status,$boxes,'discard','OPTEST-CONCORRENCIA-ROLLBACK','OPTEST-CONTENDER','$contenderRequest'::uuid);
 exception when others then get stacked diagnostics actual_state=returned_sqlstate; end;
 if actual_state<>'55P03' then raise exception 'Esperado bloqueio por concorrência; recebido %',actual_state; end if;
end `$contender`$;
select 'CONTENDER_BLOCKED';
rollback;
"@
    Write-Host 'Conexão B: tentando retirar o mesmo saldo simultaneamente...'
    $contenderOutput=Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker 'contender' $contenderSql)
    $holderOutput=Complete-PgWorker $holder
    if($contenderOutput -notmatch 'CONTENDER_BLOCKED' -or $holderOutput -notmatch 'HOLDER_ROLLED_BACK'){throw 'Confirmações de bloqueio e rollback ausentes.'}
    $retrySql=$prefix+@"

set local lock_timeout='2s';
do `$retry`$ begin
 perform public.register_stock_outflow($code,$location,$status,$boxes,'discard','OPTEST-CONCORRENCIA-ROLLBACK','OPTEST-CONTENDER','$contenderRequest'::uuid);
end `$retry`$;
select 'RETRY_SUCCEEDED';
rollback;
"@
    Write-Host 'Repetindo a tentativa após liberar o bloqueio...'
    $retryOutput=Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker 'retry' $retrySql)
    if($retryOutput -notmatch 'RETRY_SUCCEEDED'){throw 'Retomada sem confirmação.'}
    $after=(Complete-PgWorker (Start-PgWorker 'after' $snapshotSql)).Trim()|ConvertFrom-Json
    foreach($field in @('products','movements','boxes','balances_hash','movements_hash','profiles_hash')){
        if($baseline.$field -ne $after.$field){throw "A conferência final mudou em $field. Não corrigir automaticamente; revisar atividade simultânea e logs."}
    }
    $report.status='passed'
    $report['baseline']=$baseline
    $report['after']=$after
    $report['checks']=@('two_real_connections','row_lock_blocks_second_outflow','retry_after_rollback_succeeds','balances_profiles_and_history_unchanged')
    $report['limits']='Uses rollback in both connections. It does not test a committed winner followed by a rejected loser, nor real browser logins of each role.'
    Write-Host 'PASSOU: bloqueio simultâneo, retomada e preservação dos dados conferidos.'
}catch{
    $report.status='failed'
    $report['error']=$_.Exception.Message
    throw
}finally{
    foreach($worker in $workers){try{if(!$worker.Process.HasExited){$worker.Process.Kill();$worker.Process.WaitForExit(5000)|Out-Null}}catch{}; $worker.Process.Dispose()}
    $report|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6|Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $reportDirectory 'report.json') -Encoding UTF8
    if($pointer -ne [IntPtr]::Zero){[Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ZeroFreeBSTR($pointer)}
    if($secret){$secret.Dispose()}
    foreach($name in $previousEnvironment.Keys){[Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable($name,$previousEnvironment[$name],'Process')}
    Write-Host "Relatório privado: $reportDirectory"
}

import assert from 'node:assert/strict'
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
import ts from 'typescript'
const source = await readFile(new URL('../src/lib/csv.ts', import.meta.url), 'utf8')
const compiled = ts.transpileModule(source, { compilerOptions: { target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2023, module: ts.ModuleKind.ESNext } }).outputText
const { createCsv, csvCell, downloadCsv } = await import(`data:text/javascript;base64,${Buffer.from(compiled).toString('base64')}`)
assert.equal(createCsv(['Código','Aplicação','Caixas'], [['AGM-35','Corsa; "teste"\n1999',2]]), '\uFEFF"Código";"Aplicação";"Caixas"\r\n"AGM-35";"Corsa; ""teste""\n1999";2\r\n')
assert.equal(csvCell(1.25), '1,25')
assert.equal(csvCell(null), '""')
assert.equal(csvCell('a\u0000b'), '"ab"')
for (const value of ['=HYPERLINK("x")','+1','-1','@SUM(A1)',' \t=1','\n=1','\ttexto','\rteste']) assert(csvCell(value).startsWith('"\''))
assert.equal(csvCell(-1), '-1') // Um número real não é texto de cadastro interpretado como fórmula.
assert.throws(()=>csvCell(NaN));assert.throws(()=>csvCell(Infinity))
assert.throws(()=>createCsv(['A'], [[1,2]]));assert.throws(()=>createCsv([], []))
assert.equal(createCsv(['A'], []), '\uFEFF"A"\r\n')
let clicked=0, removed=0, revoked=0, savedBlob, link
const originalCreate=URL.createObjectURL, originalRevoke=URL.revokeObjectURL
URL.createObjectURL=blob=>{savedBlob=blob;return 'blob:test-csv'}
URL.revokeObjectURL=url=>{assert.equal(url,'blob:test-csv');revoked++}
globalThis.document={createElement(tag){assert.equal(tag,'a');link={click(){clicked++},remove(){removed++}};return link},body:{append(){}}}
globalThis.window={setTimeout(fn){fn()}}
downloadCsv('liber/estoque', ['Caixas'], [[53]])
assert.equal(clicked,1);assert.equal(removed,1);assert.equal(revoked,1)
assert.match(link.download,/^liber-estoque-\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\.csv$/)
assert.deepEqual([...new Uint8Array(await savedBlob.arrayBuffer()).slice(0,3)],[239,187,191])
assert.equal(await savedBlob.text(),'"Caixas"\r\n53\r\n') // Blob.text remove o BOM ao decodificar UTF-8.
document.createElement=()=>({click(){throw Error('download indisponível')},remove(){removed++}})
assert.throws(()=>downloadCsv('liber',['A'],[[1]]));assert.equal(removed,2);assert.equal(revoked,2)
URL.createObjectURL=originalCreate;URL.revokeObjectURL=originalRevoke
delete globalThis.document;delete globalThis.window
console.log('OK: CSV com acentos, separador, aspas, linhas, decimais, valores vazios, proteção de fórmulas e limpeza do download. Sem rede/banco.')

// Executa os handlers reais com hooks em memória e transporte simulado.
// Não usa credenciais, não acessa a rede e não prova o comportamento do servidor.
import assert from 'node:assert/strict'
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
import ts from 'typescript'

const root = new URL('../', import.meta.url)
const load = (source, jsx) => import(`data:text/javascript;base64,${Buffer.from(ts.transpileModule(source, {
  compilerOptions: { target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2023, module: ts.ModuleKind.ESNext, jsx, jsxFactory: 'h', jsxFragmentFactory: 'Fragment' },
}).outputText).toString('base64')}`)
let slots, cursor, transport, refresh
const hooks = {
  useState(initial) {
    const index = cursor++
    if (!(index in slots)) slots[index] = typeof initial==='function'?initial():initial
    return [slots[index], value => { slots[index] = typeof value === 'function' ? value(slots[index]) : value }]
  },
  useRef(initial) {
    const index = cursor++
    if (!(index in slots)) slots[index] = { current: initial }
    return slots[index]
  },
}
globalThis.__recovery = {
  hooks,
  client: { rpc: (...args) => transport(...args) },
  h: (type, props, ...children) => ({ type, props: props ?? {}, children: children.flat(Infinity) }),
  Fragment: 'fragment',
}

  }

  assert.equal(find('fieldset').props.disabled,true)

  await new Promise(resolve=>setImmediate(resolve))
  assert.deepEqual(reloadCalls[0],reloadCalls[1])
  assert.equal(saved.size,0)
  assert.match(text(render()),/Saída registrada/)
  props.stock=[row]
  passed('Recarga com saldo zero recupera UUID e conteúdo originais')

  reset()
  const originalStorage=window.sessionStorage
  // Confirmou a saída, mas a consulta falhou; após recarga, só consultar novamente.
  let confirmedCalls=0
  transport=async()=>{confirmedCalls++;return {data:{id:'already-confirmed'},error:null}}
  refresh=async()=>{throw new Error('Refresh unavailable')}

  await new Promise(resolve=>setImmediate(resolve))

  reset()

  await find('form').props.onSubmit(event)
  assert.equal(saved.size,0)
  assert.equal(find('fieldset').props.disabled,false)
  passed('Recusa explícita de saldo libera formulário sem pendência falsa')

  reset()
  window.sessionStorage={...originalStorage,setItem:()=>{throw new Error('Quota')}}

  await find('form').props.onSubmit(event)

import { readdirSync } from 'node:fs'
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

// Execute o build antes: check-performance valida os arquivos realmente gerados.
const checks = readdirSync(new URL('.', import.meta.url)).filter(name => /^check-.*\.mjs$/.test(name)).sort()
let failures = 0
for (const name of checks) {
  const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL(name, import.meta.url))], { stdio: 'inherit' })
  if (result.status !== 0) failures++
}
const lab = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--test', 'tests/outflow-lab/server.test.mjs'], { stdio: 'inherit' })
if (lab.status !== 0) failures++
if (failures) process.exitCode = 1

// Inicie com node scripts/serve-outflow-lab.mjs. Encerrar apaga os dados em memória.
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'
import { createServer } from 'vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import { createOutflowLab } from '../tests/outflow-lab/server.mjs'

const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../',import.meta.url))
const client = fileURLToPath(new URL('../tests/outflow-lab/client.ts',import.meta.url))
const lab = createOutflowLab()
const server = await createServer({
  root, configFile:false, envDir:false, envPrefix:'LAB_UNUSED_', publicDir:false,
  cacheDir:'node_modules/.vite-outflow-lab',
  optimizeDeps:{entries:['tests/outflow-lab/index.html']},
  server:{host:'127.0.0.1',port:5180,strictPort:true,open:false},
  plugins:[{
    name:'isolated-outflow-lab',enforce:'pre',
    resolveId(source,importer) {
      if(source==='./supabase' && importer?.replaceAll('\\','/').endsWith('/src/lib/outflow-api.ts')) return client
      // Bloqueio adicional: nunca carregar o cliente Supabase real neste servidor.
      if(source.replaceAll('\\','/').match(/(^|\/)supabase(\.ts)?$/)) throw new Error('Cliente real bloqueado no laboratório.')
    },
    configureServer(vite) {
      vite.middlewares.use((req,res,next)=>{
        // Impede conexões externas, inclusive se um import real escapar da substituição.
        res.setHeader('Content-Security-Policy',"default-src 'self'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; connect-src 'self' ws://127.0.0.1:5180; img-src 'self' data:; object-src 'none'; frame-src 'none'")
        if(req.url==='/'){res.writeHead(302,{Location:'/tests/outflow-lab/index.html'});res.end();return}
        void lab.middleware(req,res,next)
      })
    },
  },react()],
})
await server.listen()
console.log('Laboratório isolado: http://127.0.0.1:5180/tests/outflow-lab/index.html')
console.log('Sem .env, login, Supabase ou saldos reais. Dados apagados ao encerrar.')
for(const signal of ['SIGINT','SIGTERM']) process.once(signal,async()=>{await server.close();process.exit(0)})

import { test, expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test'
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright'

async function navigate(page: Page, name: string) {
  const modules: Record<string,string> = {'Painel':'dashboard','Produtos':'products','Giro e ABC':'turnover','Visão geral':'inventory','Garantia':'warranty','Molas':'springs','Saídas':'outflow','Histórico':'history','Aplicações':'catalog'}
  if ((page.viewportSize()?.width ?? 1440) <= 1024) await page.getByLabel('Escolher módulo').selectOption(modules[name])
  else if (name==='Molas'||name==='Giro e ABC') {
    await page.getByLabel('Opções da conta').click()
    await page.locator('.account-options').getByRole('button',{name,exact:true}).click()
  } else await page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Menu principal' }).getByRole('button', { name: name==='Painel'?'Painel inicial':name, exact: true }).click()
}

// Todas as chamadas ao Supabase são interceptadas: nenhuma conta ou estoque real.
const user = { id: '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111', aud: 'authenticated', role: 'authenticated', email: 'teste@example.com', app_metadata: {}, user_metadata: {}, created_at: '2026-10-27T00:00:00Z' }
const session = { access_token: 'test-access-token', refresh_token: 'test-refresh-token', token_type: 'bearer', expires_in: 3600, expires_at: Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)+3600, user }

    expect(audit.violations.map(v => ({ id: v.id, targets: v.nodes.map(n => n.target) }))).toEqual([])
  })

Funções para localizar: auth.uid, auth.

16 linhas · SHA-256 8a47cb31d20ffda3

-- SOMENTE PostgreSQL descartável. Substitutos mínimos de Auth/Storage para testar SQL.
create role anon nologin;
create role authenticated nologin;
create schema auth;
create table auth.users(id uuid primary key, email text, raw_user_meta_data jsonb default '{}'::jsonb);
create function auth.uid() returns uuid language sql stable as $$
  select nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claim.sub', true),'')::uuid
$$;
grant usage on schema auth to authenticated;
grant execute on function auth.uid() to authenticated;
alter default privileges in schema public grant select,insert,update,delete on tables to authenticated;
create schema storage;
create table storage.buckets(id text primary key,name text,public boolean,file_size_limit bigint,allowed_mime_types text[]);
create table storage.objects(id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),bucket_id text,name text);
alter table storage.objects enable row level security;

from generate_series(1,180) d cross join generate_series(1,629) n,
 public.products p,public.locations l where p.code='AGM-100' and l.code='TEST-A';

do $$ declare started timestamptz; n integer; begin
 perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub','11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',true);
 -- Reposição sintética só no banco descartável para exercitar as RPCs existentes.
 update public.stock_balances set quantity=10000 where product_id=(select id from public.products where code='AGM-100') and location_id=(select id from public.locations where code='TEST-A');

  started:=clock_timestamp();
  perform public.register_stock_outflow('AGM-100','TEST-A','released_for_sale',1,'sale',null,null,gen_random_uuid());
  insert into timings values('saida',extract(epoch from clock_timestamp()-started)*1000);
 end loop;
 for n in 1..900 loop
  started:=clock_timestamp();
  perform public.receive_stock_with_supplier('AGM-100',null,'TEST-A',1,'released_for_sale',null,null,gen_random_uuid());
  insert into timings values('entrada',extract(epoch from clock_timestamp()-started)*1000);
 end loop;
 for n in 1..20 loop
  started:=clock_timestamp();perform public.inventory_dashboard('all');
  insert into timings values('painel',extract(epoch from clock_timestamp()-started)*1000);
  started:=clock_timestamp();perform public.inventory_activity_daily('all',90);
  insert into timings values('grafico_90d',extract(epoch from clock_timestamp()-started)*1000);
 end loop;
end $$;
select json_build_object('history_rows',(select count(*) from public.stock_movements),'measurements',
 (select json_agg(t) from (select operation,count(*) as calls,round(avg(ms),3) as avg_ms,
 round(percentile_cont(.95) within group(order by ms)::numeric,3) as p95_ms from timings group by operation order by operation) t));

// Cria e apaga seu próprio PostgreSQL. Nunca usa URL/credencial de produção.
import { spawnSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process'
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'
const container = `stockflow-tests-${randomUUID()}`
const env = { ...process.env }
for (const key of ['DOCKER_HOST','DOCKER_CONTEXT','DOCKER_TLS','DOCKER_TLS_VERIFY','DOCKER_CERT_PATH']) delete env[key]
const docker = ['--host=unix:///var/run/docker.sock']
function command(args, input) {
  const result = spawnSync('docker', [...docker, ...args], { env, input, encoding: 'utf8' })
  if (result.status !== 0) throw new Error(result.stderr || result.error?.message || 'Falha do Docker')
  return result.stdout
}
const psql = ['exec','-i',container,'psql','-U','postgres','-d','stockflow_test','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1','-At']
const sql = input => command(psql, input)
function asyncSql(input) {
  return new Promise((resolve,reject) => {
    const child=spawn('docker',[...docker,...psql],{env,stdio:['pipe','pipe','pipe']})
    let output=''
    child.stdout.on('data',chunk=>{output+=chunk});child.stderr.on('data',chunk=>{output+=chunk})
    child.on('error',reject);child.on('close',code=>code===0?resolve(output):reject(new Error(output)))
    child.stdin.end(input)
  })
}
try {
  command(['run','-d','--name',container,'-e','POSTGRES_PASSWORD=isolated-test-only','-e','POSTGRES_DB=stockflow_test','postgres:17-alpine'])
  let ready=false
  for(let attempt=0;attempt<60;attempt++) {
    // O servidor temporário de inicialização só aceita socket; aguarda o TCP final.
    try { command(['exec',container,'pg_isready','-h','127.0.0.1','-U','postgres','-d','stockflow_test']);sql('select 1;');ready=true;break } catch { await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,500)) }
  }
  if(!ready)throw new Error('PostgreSQL não iniciou em 30 segundos.')
  const root=new URL('../../',import.meta.url)
  sql(readFileSync(new URL('tests/database/bootstrap.sql',root),'utf8'))
  const migrations=readdirSync(new URL('supabase/migrations/',root)).filter(name=>name.endsWith('.sql')).sort()
  for(const name of migrations)sql(readFileSync(new URL(`supabase/migrations/${name}`,root),'utf8'))
  sql(readFileSync(new URL('tests/database/transfers.sql',root),'utf8'))
  sql(readFileSync(new URL('tests/database/company-activity.sql',root),'utf8'))
  const uid='11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111'
  const request=randomUUID()
  const call=`select public.transfer_stock_once('AGM-100','TEST-A','TEST-B','released_for_sale',2,'${request}');`
  const transaction=`begin;set local role authenticated;select set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub','${uid}',true);${call}select pg_sleep(0.2);commit;`
  await Promise.all([asyncSql(transaction),asyncSql(transaction)])
  const counts=sql("select count(*) from public.stock_movements where movement_type='transfer';select sum(quantity) from public.stock_balances;select quantity from public.stock_balances b join public.locations l on l.id=b.location_id join public.products p on p.id=b.product_id where l.code='TEST-A' and p.code='AGM-100';").trim().split('\n')
  if(JSON.stringify(counts)!==JSON.stringify(['1','20','8']))throw new Error(`Concorrência alterou saldo/auditoria: ${counts}`)
  if(process.argv.includes('--capacity'))console.log('CAPACITY_RESULT '+sql(readFileSync(new URL('tests/database/capacity.sql',root),'utf8')).trim())
  console.log(`OK: ${migrations.length} migrações em PostgreSQL 17 isolado; contratos, permissões e dois reenvios simultâneos geram uma transferência e conservam 20 caixas.`)
} finally {
  command(['rm','-f',container])
}

/* oxlint-disable react/only-export-components -- entrada isolada do laboratório, fora do produto. */
import {lazy,Suspense,useState,type ReactNode} from 'react'
import {createRoot} from 'react-dom/client'
import {ModuleBoundary,ModuleLoading} from '../../src/components/ModuleBoundary'
import '../../src/index.css'

let complete:()=>void
const Slow=lazy(()=>new Promise<{default:()=>ReactNode}>(resolve=>{
  complete=()=>resolve({default:()=> <h2>Tela carregada sem movimentar estoque</h2>})
}))
const Broken=lazy(()=>Promise.reject(new Error('Falha simulada no módulo, sem rede ou banco')))
function Lab(){
  const [page,setPage]=useState('home')
  return <main style={{maxWidth:800,margin:'40px auto',padding:24}}>
    <h1>Laboratório de carregamento</h1><p>Componentes reais de espera e tratamento de erro, sem Supabase, sessão ou estoque.</p>
    <nav aria-label="Telas de teste"><button onClick={()=>setPage('home')}>Página inicial</button> <button onClick={()=>setPage('slow')}>Abrir tela lenta</button> <button onClick={()=>complete?.()}>Concluir carregamento</button> <button onClick={()=>setPage('broken')}>Simular falha</button></nav>
    <ModuleBoundary key={page}><Suspense fallback={<ModuleLoading/>}>{page==='slow'?<Slow/>:page==='broken'?<Broken/>:<h2>Página inicial disponível</h2>}</Suspense></ModuleBoundary>
  </main>
}
createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(<Lab/> )

16 linhas · SHA-256 d4b684697624e330

// Substitui somente o transporte no servidor local de laboratório.
// O componente e a validação de saídas continuam sendo os arquivos reais do projeto.
export const supabase = {
  async rpc(functionName: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/__outflow_lab/rpc', {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ functionName, payload }),
      })
      return await response.json()
    } catch {
      return { data: null, error: { code: 'NETWORK', message: 'Conexão do laboratório interrompida.' } }
    }
  },
}

Funções para localizar: Lab, restart, resume, read.

63 linhas · SHA-256 19b84ce60e3e1d64

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client'
import { StockOutflows } from '../../src/components/StockOutflows'
import type { StockRow } from '../../src/lib/inventory-api'
import './style.css'

type LabState = { balances: Record<string,number>; events: {id:string;code:string;requestId:string}[]; attempts: {requestId:string;code:string;repeated:boolean;responseLost?:boolean}[] }
export function Lab() {
  const [state,setState] = useState<LabState|null>(null)
  const [stock,setStock] = useState<StockRow[]>([])
  const [error,setError] = useState('')
  async function restart() {
    try {
      // Apaga somente a memória do laboratório, antes de uma nova rodada.
      const response=await fetch('/__outflow_lab/reset',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:'{}'})
      if(!response.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao reiniciar laboratório.')
      // A conta fictícia do laboratório nunca compartilha pedidos com o site real.
      sessionStorage.removeItem('liber.outflow.pending.v1:lab-administrator')
      window.location.reload()
    } catch {setError('Não foi possível reiniciar o laboratório local.')}
  }
  async function resume(code:string) {
    try {
      const response=await fetch('/__outflow_lab/resume',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({code})})
      if(!response.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao restabelecer respostas.')
    } catch {setError('Não foi possível restabelecer as respostas locais.')}
  }
  async function read(updateStock = false) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/__outflow_lab/state',{cache:'no-store'})
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Falha na consulta local.')
      const next:LabState = await response.json()
      setState(next);setError('')
      if(updateStock) setStock(Object.entries(next.balances).filter(([,boxes])=>boxes>0).map(([code,boxes])=>({
        code,location:'LAB',status:code==='MGM-35'?'Em estoque':'Liberado para venda',boxes,
      })) as StockRow[])
    } catch { setError('Não foi possível consultar o servidor local do laboratório.'); if(updateStock) throw new Error('Consulta local falhou.') }
  }
  useEffect(()=>{
    // A sincronização faz fetch; os setters só executam após a resposta assíncrona.
    // oxlint-disable-next-line react/set-state-in-effect
    void read(true).catch(()=>{})
    // A observação não atualiza o saldo do formulário durante uma confirmação incerta.
    const timer=setInterval(()=>void read(),800)
    return ()=>clearInterval(timer)
  },[])
  return <main>
    <header><p>LABORATÓRIO LOCAL · SEM SUPABASE</p><h1>Perda de confirmação</h1>
      <p>Dados fictícios em memória. A retirada será aceita pelo servidor local, mas suas respostas serão interrompidas até clicar em Restabelecer respostas. Depois reenvie sem mudar os campos.</p>
      <p>O perfil de Administrador aqui é simulado. Este ambiente não testa login ou permissões reais.</p></header>
    {error&&<p role="alert">{error}</p>}
    {state&&<aside aria-label="Estado no servidor de teste"><h2>Servidor local</h2>
      <p>Amortecedores: <strong>{state.balances['AGM-35']}</strong> caixas · Molas: <strong>{state.balances['MGM-35']}</strong> caixas · Retiradas registradas: <strong>{state.events.length}</strong></p>
      <button onClick={()=>void resume('AGM-35')}>Restabelecer respostas de amortecedores</button>{' '}
      <button onClick={()=>void resume('MGM-35')}>Restabelecer respostas de molas</button>
      {' '}<button onClick={()=>void restart()}>Reiniciar somente os dados do laboratório</button>
      <ol>{state.attempts.map((attempt,index)=><li key={index}>{attempt.code} · ID {attempt.requestId} · {attempt.responseLost?'Retirada aceita; confirmação cortada':attempt.repeated?'Reenvio: mesmo evento, sem nova retirada':'Resposta entregue'}</li>)}</ol>
    </aside>}
    <StockOutflows userId="lab-administrator" stock={stock} catalog={{}} ready={Boolean(state)} isAdministrator={true} onRefresh={()=>read(true)}/>
  </main>
}
createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(<Lab/> )

Funções para localizar: createOutflowLab, reset.

// Servidor de laboratório: tudo vive em memória. Não importa Supabase ou .env.
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'

export function createOutflowLab() {
  let balances, events, attempts, restored
  function reset() {
    balances = { 'AGM-35': 2, 'MGM-35': 2 }
    events = new Map()
    attempts = []
    restored = new Set()
  }
  reset()
  const state = () => ({ balances: { ...balances }, events: [...events.values()], attempts: [...attempts] })
  const json = (res, status, value) => {
    res.writeHead(status, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' })
    res.end(JSON.stringify(value))
  }
  return {
    state,
    async middleware(req, res, next) {
      const path = req.url?.split('?')[0]
      if (!path?.startsWith('/__outflow_lab/')) return next()
      if (path === '/__outflow_lab/state' && req.method === 'GET') return json(res, 200, state())
      // O laboratório só aceita escrita da própria página local.
      if (req.method !== 'POST' || req.headers.origin !== `http://${req.headers.host}` || req.headers['content-type'] !== 'application/json') {
        return json(res, 403, { error: 'Requisição fora do laboratório.' })
      }
      try {
        let body = ''
        for await (const chunk of req) {
          body += chunk
          if (Buffer.byteLength(body) > 16384) return json(res, 413, { error: 'Corpo muito grande.' })
        }
        if (path === '/__outflow_lab/reset') { reset(); return json(res, 200, state()) }
        if (path === '/__outflow_lab/resume') {
          const { code } = JSON.parse(body)
          if (!Object.hasOwn(balances,code)) return json(res,400,{error:'Categoria inválida.'})
          restored.add(code)
          return json(res,200,{restored:code})
        }
        if (path !== '/__outflow_lab/rpc') return json(res, 404, { error: 'Rota ausente.' })
        const { functionName, payload: p } = JSON.parse(body)
        if (functionName !== 'register_stock_outflow' || !p || !Object.hasOwn(balances, p.p_code)
          || p.p_location !== 'LAB' || p.p_status !== (p.p_code === 'MGM-35' ? 'in_stock' : 'released_for_sale')
          || p.p_reason !== 'sale' || !Number.isInteger(p.p_boxes) || p.p_boxes < 1
          || !/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/i.test(p.p_request_id ?? '')) {
          return json(res, 400, { data: null, error: { code: '22023' } })
        }
        const fingerprint = JSON.stringify([p.p_code,p.p_location,p.p_status,p.p_boxes,p.p_reason,p.p_notes,p.p_reference])
        const existing = events.get(p.p_request_id)
        attempts.push({ requestId: p.p_request_id, code: p.p_code, boxes: p.p_boxes, repeated: Boolean(existing) })
        if (existing) {
          if (existing.fingerprint !== fingerprint) return json(res, 400, { data: null, error: { code: '22023' } })
          if (!restored.has(p.p_code)) { attempts.at(-1).responseLost=true; res.destroy(); return }
          return json(res, 200, { data: { id: existing.id }, error: null })
        }
        if (balances[p.p_code] < p.p_boxes) return json(res, 409, { data: null, error: { code: 'P0001' } })
        // Este é o COMMIT simulado em memória, antes de cortar a conexão HTTP real.
        balances[p.p_code] -= p.p_boxes
        const saved = { id: randomUUID(), requestId: p.p_request_id, code: p.p_code, boxes: p.p_boxes, fingerprint }
        events.set(p.p_request_id, saved)
        if (!restored.has(p.p_code)) {
          attempts.at(-1).responseLost = true
          res.destroy() // Sem cabeçalhos ou confirmação: o navegador recebe falha de rede.
          return
        }
        return json(res, 200, { data: { id: saved.id }, error: null })
      } catch {
        if (!res.destroyed) json(res, 400, { error: 'Requisição inválida.' })
      }
    },
  }
}
tests/outflow-lab/server.test.mjs

Ensaio isolado: cria dados/respostas de teste para verificar comportamentos. Não deve ser usado como uma migração do banco de produção.

41 linhas · SHA-256 b23ad3d139c460b8

// Verifica o isolamento e a falha HTTP real do servidor descartável.
import { test } from 'node:test'
import assert from 'node:assert/strict'
import { createServer } from 'node:http'
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'
import { createOutflowLab } from './server.mjs'

test('laboratório corta a resposta após aceite e não duplica o pedido',async()=>{
  const lab=createOutflowLab()
  const server=createServer((req,res)=>void lab.middleware(req,res,()=>{res.writeHead(404);res.end()}))
  await new Promise(resolve=>server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',resolve))
  const origin=`http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`
  const post=(path,value,requestOrigin=origin)=>fetch(origin+path,{
    method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json',Origin:requestOrigin},body:JSON.stringify(value),
  })
  try{
    assert.equal((await post('/__outflow_lab/reset',{},'http://outro-site.invalid')).status,403)
    for(const code of ['AGM-35','MGM-35']){
      const payload={p_code:code,p_location:'LAB',p_status:code==='MGM-35'?'in_stock':'released_for_sale',p_boxes:1,p_reason:'sale',p_notes:null,p_reference:null,p_request_id:randomUUID()}
      const body={functionName:'register_stock_outflow',payload}
      await assert.rejects(post('/__outflow_lab/rpc',body),/fetch failed/)
      assert.equal(lab.state().balances[code],1)
      await assert.rejects(post('/__outflow_lab/rpc',body),/fetch failed/)
      await post('/__outflow_lab/resume',{code})
      const retry=await (await post('/__outflow_lab/rpc',body)).json()
      assert(retry.data.id)
      assert.equal(lab.state().balances[code],1)
      assert.equal(lab.state().events.filter(event=>event.code===code).length,1)
      const changed=await post('/__outflow_lab/rpc',{...body,payload:{...payload,p_boxes:2}})
      assert.equal((await changed.json()).error.code,'22023')
    }
    assert.equal(lab.state().events.length,2)
    const separate=createOutflowLab()
    assert.equal(separate.state().events.length,0)
    assert.deepEqual(separate.state().balances,{'AGM-35':2,'MGM-35':2})
  }finally{
    server.closeAllConnections()
    await new Promise(resolve=>server.close(resolve))
  }
})

Arquivo de infraestrutura ou ferramenta de apoio. Leia os comentários e consulte a aula sobre pastas para entender onde ele participa do build, dos testes ou da publicação.

name: Verificar projeto

on:
  push:
  pull_request:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: verify-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  verify:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '24'
          cache: npm
      - run: npm ci
      - name: Build e regressões
        run: npm test
      - name: Análise do código
        run: npm run lint
      - name: Preparar navegador
        run: npx playwright install --with-deps chromium
      - name: Testar navegador com dados simulados
        run: npm run test:browser
      - name: Testar banco descartável
        run: npm run test:database
      - name: Guardar diagnóstico de falhas no navegador
        if: failure()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: browser-test-results
          path: test-results/
          if-no-files-found: ignore
          retention-days: 7
